### [1] [Synthesis Through Simulation: Generating Coherent Enterprise Data via Scalable Agent-System Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10549)
*Yipeng Li,Ashutosh Hathidara,Jane Lo,Harshavardhan Abichandani,Gunraj Singh,Atin Ghosh*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出通过模拟环境与工具调用API合成企业数据的agent框架，对agent工具反馈与训练环境构建有迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出 STS 无模式数据合成范式，通过模拟企业环境中的策略执行 API 生成数据；GP 智能体在十个环境上实现 0.88 平均边际保真度和 100% 约束满足，并开源框架、环境与数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业 AI 中工具调用智能体的大规模训练与评估受商业和法律限制，现有表格数据合成受结构有效性和模式可用性制约，而基于过程的方法又缺乏分布保真度。

Method: STS 让 LLM 智能体在模拟企业环境中针对策略执行 API 执行操作来生成数据，从构造上保证结构有效性，并将有效性执行与分布建模解耦；领域无关的 GP 负责分布保真度和合成可扩展性。

Result: GP 无需数据库模式，在全部十个环境上取得 0.88 平均边际保真度和 100% 约束满足；统计合成器因种子数据要求对七个环境不适用；拥有模式权限的智能体在航空公司环境紧耦合工作流中 82% 轨迹失败。

Conclusion: STS 为受限企业场景下的大规模智能体训练与评估提供了可行合成路径，并开源完整框架、十个环境及生成数据集。

中文摘要：工具调用智能体已成为企业 AI 的核心，但由于对企业系统、数据和数据库模式的商业与法律限制，大规模训练和评估它们仍受到严重制约。表格数据合成提供了一种自然的替代方案，但其有效性从根本上受限于结构有效性和模式可用性；而基于过程的方法则表现出相反的弱点，通常在没有逐领域编写的情况下缺乏分布保真度。我们提出“通过模拟合成”（Synthesis Through Simulation, STS），这是一种无模式的数据合成范式，其中 LLM 智能体通过在模拟企业环境中针对执行策略的 API 执行操作来生成数据。由于数据是通过定义何为有效的同一环境生成的，STS 从构造上保证结构有效性，同时将有效性强制执行与分布建模解耦，使二者可以独立处理。STS 的领域无关智能体“通用填充器”（Generalist Populator, GP）解决了剩余的分布保真度和合成可扩展性挑战：GP 在无法访问数据库模式的情况下，在全部十个环境中实现了 0.88 的平均边际保真度和 100% 的约束满足率，而统计合成器由于必要的种子数据要求，对其中七个环境不适用；拥有模式权限的智能体则因任务组合脆弱，在航空公司环境的紧耦合工作流上于 82% 的轨迹中失败。我们在 https://github.com/SAP/synthesis-through-simulation 开源了完整框架、全部十个环境以及生成的数据集。

Original abstract: Tool-calling agents have become central to enterprise AI, yet training and evaluating them at scale remains severely constrained due to business and legal restrictions on enterprise systems, data, and database schemas. Tabular data synthesis offers a natural alternative, but its effectiveness is fundamentally limited by structural validity and schema availability, while procedure-based approaches yield the opposite weakness, typically lacking distributional fidelity without per-domain authoring. We introduce **Synthesis Through Simulation** (STS), a **schema--free** data synthesis paradigm in which an LLM agent generates data by executing operations against policy-enforcing APIs within simulated enterprise environments. Because data is generated through the same environment that defines what is valid, STS guarantees structural validity by construction while decoupling validity enforcement from distribution modeling, allowing each to be addressed independently. The **Generalist Populator** (GP), STS's domain-agnostic agent, addresses the remaining challenges of distributional fidelity and synthesis scalability: GP achieves **0.88** average marginal fidelity and **100\% constraint satisfaction** across all ten environments *without access to DB schemas*, while statistical synthesizers are inapplicable to seven due to necessary seed data requirements, and schema-privileged agents fail 82\% of trajectories on airline environment's tightly coupled workflows due to brittle task composition. We open-source the full framework, all ten environments, and generated datasets at https://github.com/SAP/synthesis-through-simulation.

</details>


### [2] [Agent-Controlled Forgetting for Tool-Using Agents: Reversible Context Curation in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10590)
*Jan-Peter Franke*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 13 pages, 1 figure. Code and research artifacts: https://github.com/boldprojekte/agent-forgetting-research

Selection reason: 研究tool-using agent的可逆上下文遗忘与记忆管理，直接对应agent长程执行中的上下文策展与harness设计。

TL;DR: 研究智能体控制的可逆遗忘：将历史工具结果原位替换为短注释并保留原文归档。在OpenTelemetry调试案例中显著降低token和API成本，但请求更多、耗时更长；在应用开发案例中无节省。结论：资源节省依赖工作负载，可逆上下文管理需考虑任务类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用智能体会累积大量低价值工具观察，导致上下文膨胀和成本上升，需要一种由智能体控制、可逆的上下文管理机制。

Method: 行动模型选择先前工具结果，将每条原位替换为短注释，原文存入可恢复档案；Python测试框架支持批量归档与显式恢复，无需任务特定训练，并保护用户指令和助手消息。

Result: OpenTelemetry调试加无关实现任务中，最终提示词token为231,951对912,492，累计输入token少50%，估计API成本1.28–1.44美元对约4.38美元；两组均通过主要行为判定器，但均未完全通过后续评估；方法请求更多、耗时多17%。对比应用开发任务无上下文或成本节省，早先延续人工质量较低。

Conclusion: 该方法在高噪声工具使用轨迹中可显著节省资源，但效果依赖工作负载；可逆上下文管理应将工作负载依赖性作为核心考量。

中文摘要：使用工具的智能体反复携带观察结果，这些观察结果的有用内容可能远小于其原始负载。我们研究由智能体控制的遗忘：行动模型选择先前观察到的工具结果，将每个结果在其原始位置替换为一条简短注释，并将精确原文保留在可恢复档案中。一个Python测试框架提供批量归档和显式恢复，无需任务特定的模型训练，同时保护用户指令和助手消息不受这些操作影响。在一个探索性OpenTelemetry调试案例随后接一个无关实现任务中，该方法最终提供方报告的提示词token为231,951，而保留历史为912,492；累计输入token减少50%；估计API成本为1.28–1.44美元，而约为4.38美元。两个分支均通过了这两个案例的主要行为判定器；两者均未完全满足后续评估。该方法发出了更多请求，并且耗时长了17%。一个对比性的应用开发配对没有产生上下文或成本节省，而一个较早的延续尽管上下文减少，但人工评估质量较低。这些观察表明，在噪声工具使用轨迹中可大幅节省资源，并将工作负载依赖性确定为可逆上下文管理的核心考量因素。

Original abstract: Tool-using agents repeatedly carry observations whose useful content can be much smaller than their original payload. We study agent-controlled forgetting: the acting model selects previously observed tool results, replaces each with a short note at its original position, and retains the exact original in a recoverable archive. A Python harness exposes batch archival and explicit recovery without task-specific model training, while protecting user instructions and assistant messages from these operations. In an exploratory OpenTelemetry debugging case followed by an unrelated implementation task, the method ended with 231,951 provider-reported prompt tokens versus 912,492 under retained history, used 50% fewer cumulative input tokens, and had an estimated API cost of USD 1.28-1.44 versus approximately USD 4.38. Both arms passed the two-case primary behavioral oracle; neither fully satisfied the follow-up evaluation. The method made more requests and took 17% longer. A contrasting application-development pair produced no context or cost saving, and an earlier continuation exhibited lower manually assessed quality despite reduced context. These observations demonstrate substantial resource savings in noisy tool-use trajectories and identify workload dependence as a central consideration for reversible context management.

</details>


### [3] [Verification and Self-Improvement in Agentic AI: Foundations and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10611)
*Chien-Ping Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 26 pages, 5 figures. Includes proofs and reproducibility artifacts

Selection reason: 从理论层面分析agentic AI的验证与自改进边界，对agent可靠性、验证协议设计有启发。

TL;DR: 提出带隐藏终端随机性的有界验证框架，用于区分智能体AI的搜索、支持与输出修改等改进机制，并刻画随机验证类及自我改进的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性能分数无法区分搜索更久、获得额外支持或修改提出/验证输出等机制，因此需要可比较、可约束的验证框架。

Method: 定义阶段（可接受记录、多项式界、交替验证协议、终端检查器），区分原生可达与闭包前沿；在逐点概率间隙和任务相对可靠性下证明性质；分析独立多数放大、随机纸带存在接受、精确验证、随机验证类、表示分析、递归自我修改、条件误差预算、XOR合成族与审计。

Result: 随机验证类满足 Σ_k^P⊆Σ_k^RV⊆Σ_{k+1}^P；严格扩大和深度分离需显式复杂性假设；BPP=P 时精确伴随类具有相同前沿；多数放大保持语言而随机纸带存在接受可产生错误输出；有界自修改仍在同一验证类；XOR合成族分离搜索成功与语言变化。

Conclusion: 该框架将自我改进主张转化为对正确性、可接受证据、验证资源和选择错误的明确义务。

中文摘要：智能体式人工智能系统可以通过更长时间搜索、获得额外支持，或修改其提出并验证输出的方式来改进。性能分数无法区分这些机制。我们通过带有隐藏终端随机性的有界验证来比较这些变化。一个阶段指定可接受记录、多项式界、交替验证协议和终端检查器。其原生可达范围使用默认支持；其闭包前沿允许接口已经接纳的所有支持。在一致逐点概率间隙和任务相对可靠性下，这些是定义良好的语言。我们证明，独立多数放大保持这两种语言，而对随机纸带的存在性接受可能接纳不正确的输出。精确验证是零随机性情形，并给出定位与完备性结果。随机验证器类满足 Σ_k^P⊆Σ_k^RV⊆Σ_{k+1}^P；严格扩大和深度分离需要显式的复杂性假设，而 BPP=P 产生具有相同前沿的精确伴随类。表示分析将不变接受与在重构下可能改变的核心—支持标签区分开来。对于递归自我改进，在共同可靠解释器和固定验证协议下的均匀有界自我修改仍保持在同一个验证类内。一个单独的条件误差预算控制在自适应选择的候选者之间的错误选择。一个配额强制的 XOR 合成族将搜索成功的无界比率与已接受语言的变化分离开来；精确审计和概率审计检查由此产生的证据要求。该框架将自我改进主张与关于正确性、可接受证据、验证资源和选择错误的义务联系起来。

Original abstract: Agentic AI systems can improve by searching longer, receiving additional support, or modifying how they propose and verify outputs. A performance score does not distinguish these mechanisms. We compare these changes through bounded verification with hidden terminal randomness. A stage specifies admissible transcripts, polynomial bounds, an alternating verification protocol, and a terminal checker. Its native reach uses default support; its closure frontier permits all support already admitted by the interface. Under a uniform pointwise probability gap and task-relative soundness, these are well-defined languages. We prove that independent majority amplification preserves both languages, whereas existential acceptance over random tapes can admit incorrect outputs. Exact verification is the zero-randomness case, with placement and completeness results. The randomized-verifier classes satisfy $Σ_k^{\mathrm{P}}\subseteqΣ_k^{\mathrm{RV}}\subseteqΣ_{k+1}^{\mathrm{P}}$; strict enlargement and depth separation require explicit complexity assumptions, while $\mathrm{BPP}=\mathrm{P}$ yields exact companions with the same frontiers. Representation analysis separates invariant acceptance from core-versus-support labels that can change under refactoring. For recursive self-improvement, uniformly bounded self-modification under a common sound interpreter and fixed verification protocol remains within the same verification class. A separate conditional-error budget controls false selection across adaptively chosen candidates. A quota-enforced XOR-synthesis family separates unbounded ratios of search success from changes in the accepted languages; exact and probabilistic audits check the resulting evidence requirements. The framework ties self-improvement claims to obligations on correctness, admissible evidence, verification resources, and selection error.

</details>


### [4] [The Harness as the Only Mutable Surface: Compliance-Bounded Self-Evolution of LLM Agents in Credit Pipelines, with a Measured Admission Gate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10629)
*Ravil Akhtyamov*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 14 pages, 5 tables. Code, configuration and per-run outputs: https://github.com/rav11l/cace-bench (v0.6.0, doi:10.5281/zenodo.23207546)

Selection reason: 将可审查自进化限制在runtime harness并提出准入闸门，直接关联agent自我改进与安全控制设计。

TL;DR: 该文主张LLM智能体自我演化应仅限运行时框架且冻结权重，以保证可审查性；通过带哈希链记录准入闸门的双环引擎仿真，闸门在7,449个候选中仅接纳144个且无留出集错误恶化，而无界检查接纳309个有害变更并导致高漏报；偏移前标签上全部拒绝，结构性偏移最难，最高严重度下闸门容差阻碍正确替换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我重写会破坏监督审查所需的具名变更、测试记录和批准；需要使自我演化可审查。

Method: 限定自我演化于运行时框架（指令、工具调用逻辑、基本组件组合），冻结模型权重；构建双环引擎与部署前写入哈希链记录的准入闸门；在仿真中用模拟智能体和种子搜索提议器，跨三类监督式重新解释、三种严重度、每类10种子进行评估。

Result: 带闸门时，7,449个候选接纳144个，无留出历史错误恶化，低/中严重度假阳性恢复至预言机且不增漏报。替换为无界检查时，接纳309个有害变更，90次中49次漏报高于10%。偏移前标签上闸门全部拒绝。参数与范围偏移局部修复，结构性偏移需基本组件替换；最高结构严重度下固定容差在一半种子中阻止正确替换。

Conclusion: 可审查的自我演化必须编码为可重新标注历史的规则并限制在运行时框架内；机制对应《欧盟人工智能法案》高风险信用评分条款，而2026年4月美国模型风险指南将智能体AI排除在范围外。

中文摘要：自我改进的LLM智能体能够使信用流水线适应规则变化，但一个重写自身的智能体会破坏监督者所审查的工件：一项具名变更、一次记录的测试、一次批准。我们认为，只有当自我演化被限制在运行时框架（指令文本、工具调用逻辑与基本组件组合）之内，而模型权重保持固定时，自我演化才是可审查的，这样每次适应都是一个带有原因和附带测试的差异（diff）。我们基于这一界限给出一个双环引擎，其中包含一个准入闸门，在部署前写入一条哈希链式记录；我们在仿真中度量该闸门，使用的是模拟智能体和种子搜索提议器，而非语言模型。在三大类监督式重新解释、三种严重程度、每种10个种子下，带闸门的环路在7,449个候选变更中接纳了144个，其中没有一个在留出历史上使错误恶化，并且在每个低和中严重程度单元中，将假阳性率恢复到预言机水平，同时没有提高漏报。将闸门替换为无界系统所采用的检查（近期轨迹中可见的错误更少）后，同样的环路接纳了309个有害变更，并在90次运行中的49次留下高于10%的漏报：假阳性下降是因为筛查被放松了。在偏移前标签上评估时，闸门拒绝了每一个候选，因此重新解释必须被编码为一条重新标注历史的规则。参数偏移和范围偏移被局部修复，结构性偏移只能通过基本组件替换来修复；在最高结构性严重程度下，闸门的固定容差在一半的种子中阻止了正确的替换。我们将这些机制映射到《欧盟人工智能法案》关于高风险信用评分的条款，并指出2026年4月美国模型风险指南将智能体AI排除在其范围之外。

Original abstract: Self-improving LLM agents can adapt a credit pipeline to a changed rule, but an agent that rewrites itself destroys the artefact a supervisor reviews: a named change, a recorded test, an approval. We argue that self-evolution is reviewable only if it is confined to the runtime harness (instruction text, tool-call logic and primitive composition) while model weights stay fixed, so that every adaptation is a diff with a cause and a test attached. We give a dual-loop engine built on that bound, with one admission gate that writes a hash-chained record before deployment, and we measure the gate in simulation, with a simulated agent and a seeded-search proposer rather than language models. Across three families of supervisory re-interpretation at three severities, 10 seeds each, the gated loop admitted 144 of 7,449 candidate changes, none of which worsened error on held-out history, and restored the false-positive rate to the oracle level without raising missed flags in every low- and mid-severity cell. With the gate replaced by the check an unbounded system applies (fewer errors visible in recent traces), the same loops admitted 309 harmful changes and left missed flags above 10% in 49 of 90 runs: false positives fell because the screen was loosened. Evaluated on pre-shift labels, the gate rejected every candidate, so a re-interpretation must be encoded as a rule that relabels history. Parametric and scope shifts were repaired locally, a structural one only by primitive replacement; at the highest structural severity the gate's fixed tolerance blocked the correct replacement in half the seeds. We map the mechanisms to the EU AI Act's provisions for high-risk credit scoring and note that the April 2026 US model-risk guidance excludes agentic AI from its scope.

</details>


### [5] [Plan-and-Patch: Diffusion Language Models for Agentic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10786)
*Syamantak Kumar,Jiang Guo,Hassan Hamad,Hideo Kobayashi,Yi Xiang,Yezhou Yang,Yanjun Qi,Daniele Bonadiman,Jiarong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出基于扩散语言模型的Plan-and-Patch规划与局部修复框架，可迁移到长程agent的规划与重规划。

TL;DR: 提出 Plan-and-Patch 计划与行动框架，利用扩散语言模型生成结构化计划并局部修复，在长时程智能体中实现更快计划生成和更高修复成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体规划中，环境变化、工具意外返回和动作失败会推翻原有假设，因此需要有效修订计划；而修订通常只涉及局部，重新生成完整计划可能引入不必要改动。

Method: 使用扩散语言模型通过并行解掩码生成程序式结构化计划，并在保留前缀和后缀的前提下填充受影响的局部区域以完成计划修复；与自回归规划器 DreamReasoner-8B 和 Qwen3-8B 进行比较，并在 Natural Plan、ALFWorld 和 TextCraft 上评估。

Result: 在 Natural Plan 无任务特定训练时，扩散规划器计划修复成功率为 53.7%，接近自回归的 27.0% 的两倍；在 ALFWorld 和 TextCraft 任务特定训练后，两者计划生成观测成功率相似，但扩散将平均计划生成延迟降低 39-46%。

Conclusion: Plan-and-Patch 为长时程智能体提供了一种更快生成计划并进行有效计划修复的框架。

中文摘要：规划对于长时程智能体日益重要，成功执行需要在许多步骤中协调子目标、工具使用和中间结果。然而，规划期间所做的假设可能被环境推翻，工具可能返回意外结果，或者动作可能失败。因此，有效的智能体不仅必须生成计划，还必须修订计划。此类修订通常只影响计划的一部分，使之前和之后的结构保持不变。与其重新生成整个计划并冒不必要更改的风险，修复可以基于保留的前缀和后缀重新生成受影响的区域。我们提出 Plan-and-Patch，一个计划与行动框架，其中扩散语言模型（dLLM）通过并行解掩码生成结构化的、类程序计划，并通过填充选定区域同时保持周围步骤不变来修复计划。我们将 DreamReasoner-8B 和 Qwen3-8B 分别作为扩散和自回归（AR）规划器进行比较。在 Natural Plan 上，无需任务特定训练，扩散（53.7%）的计划修复成功率几乎是 AR（27.0%）的两倍。在智能体基准 ALFWorld 和 TextCraft 上进行任务特定训练后，规划器在计划生成上取得相似的观测成功率，而扩散相对于 AR 将平均计划生成延迟降低 39-46%。我们的结果表明，Plan-and-Patch 为长时程智能体中的更快计划生成和有效计划修复提供了一个框架。

Original abstract: Planning is increasingly important for long-horizon agents, where successful execution requires coordinating subgoals, tool use, and intermediate outcomes over many steps. Yet assumptions made during planning may be invalidated by the environment, tools may return unexpected results, or actions may fail. Effective agents must therefore not only generate plans, but also revise them. Such revisions often affect only part of a plan, leaving the preceding and subsequent structure intact. Rather than regenerate the entire plan and risk unnecessary changes, repair can regenerate the affected region conditioned on the preserved prefix and suffix. We introduce Plan-and-Patch, a plan-and-act framework in which a diffusion language model (dLLM) generates a structured, program-like plan through parallel unmasking and repairs it by filling in selected regions while keeping the surrounding steps fixed. We compare DreamReasoner-8B and Qwen3-8B as diffusion and autoregressive (AR) planners. On Natural Plan without task-specific training, diffusion (53.7%) achieves nearly twice the plan repair success rate of AR (27.0%). After task-specific training on agentic benchmarks, ALFWorld and TextCraft, the planners achieve similar observed success in plan generation, while diffusion reduces mean plan-generation latency by 39-46% relative to AR. Our results show that Plan-and-Patch provides a framework for faster plan generation and effective plan repair in long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [6] [On the Clock: Towards Punctual and Productive Time-Budgeted AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10833)
*Aaron Wang,Neelabh Madan,Vlad Sobal,Matthew Trager,Michael Kleinman,Elman Mansimov,Wei Xia,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Resource-Aware Agentic AI. 23 pages

Selection reason: 研究时间预算下agent的harness计时反馈与RL预算感知训练，对agent执行控制与资源约束有参考价值。

TL;DR: 研究小型LLM智能体在墙钟时间预算下能否既遵守时间又有效利用时间，发现时间遵守与产出性时间分配之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究小型LLM智能体在显式墙钟时间预算下是否能兼顾遵守运行时间与有效利用可用时间。

Method: 在MLE-Bench Lite五个竞赛上评估Qwen3.6-27B，在Zork I (Jericho)上评估Qwen3-4B，并比较基于执行框架的计时信息暴露与截止强制执行，以及带预算感知奖励的强化学习（GRPO）。

Result: 仅提示预算时智能体无法受控使用时间；注入计时信息可提升Qwen3.6-27B预算遵守且不损性能，强制执行钩子进一步收紧；GRPO在Zork I近完美遵守并泛化到留出预算，但在MLE-Bench无任务性能提升；智能体仍不能利用额外时间，常以重复动作填充或坍缩到最短预算策略。

Conclusion: 时间遵守与有效时间分配之间存在核心差距，这是预算条件智能体仍面临的关键挑战。

中文摘要：我们研究小型LLM智能体能否在明确的墙钟时间预算下有效运作，既遵守分配到的运行时间，又有效利用可用时间。我们在MLE-Bench Lite的五个竞赛上评估Qwen3.6-27B，并在Zork I (Jericho)上评估Qwen3-4B；这两个智能体基准中，额外计算时间能够显著提升性能。在最简单设置中，即预算仅在提示中说明时，智能体未能将所述预算转化为对时间的受控使用。这些失败源于时间意识缺口，因为执行框架不提供计时反馈；但也因为它们无法可靠预判动作的持续时间，并且没有从可用时间到适当策略的学习映射。我们研究两类互补干预：基于执行框架的机制，用于暴露计时信息并强制执行截止时间；以及带预算感知奖励的强化学习。通过执行框架注入计时信息，显著提升了Qwen3.6-27B的预算遵守度，且没有可测的性能损失，而强制执行钩子进一步收紧遵守度。使用GRPO的强化学习在Zork I上达到近乎完美的预算遵守，并能泛化到训练中未见过的留出预算，但在MLE-Bench上相比未训练的执行框架并未提升任务性能。一旦智能体被要求遵守预算，它们仍未能利用额外时间来提升任务性能。经RL训练的策略学会了何时停止，但常常用重复动作填充额外时间，而在多个预算上进行GRPO训练则倾向于坍缩到为最短预算学到的策略。我们的结果揭示了时间遵守与有效时间分配之间的差距，这仍是预算条件智能体的核心挑战。

Original abstract: We study whether small LLM agents can operate effectively under explicit wall-clock time budgets by both respecting the allocated runtime and using available time productively. We evaluate Qwen3.6-27B on five competitions from MLE-Bench Lite and Qwen3-4B on Zork I (Jericho), two agentic benchmarks where additional computational time can meaningfully improve performance. In the simplest setting, where the budget is stated only in the prompt, agents fail to translate the stated budget into controlled use of time. These failures arise from gaps in time awareness, since the harness provides no timing feedback, but also because they cannot reliably anticipate the duration of actions, and do not have a learned mapping from available time to an appropriate strategy. We investigate two complementary classes of interventions: harness-based mechanisms that expose timing information and enforce deadlines, and reinforcement learning with budget-aware rewards. Injecting timing information through the harness substantially improves budget adherence for Qwen3.6-27B without measurable loss in performance, while enforcement hooks tighten adherence further. RL with GRPO achieves near-perfect budget adherence on Zork I and generalizes to held-out budgets not seen during training, but does not improve task performance over the untrained harness on MLE-Bench. Once agents are made to respect the budget, they still fail to use additional time to improve task performance. RL-trained policies learn when to stop but often fill extra time with repeated actions, and GRPO training on multiple budgets tends to collapse toward the strategy learned for the shortest budget. Our results reveal a gap between time adherence and productive time allocation, which remains a central challenge for budget-conditioned agents.

</details>


### [7] [Reading the Room: Foundations, Design, and Challenges of Normative Competence in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10906)
*Andrea Wynn,Harsh Satija,Seokhyun,Baek,Anqi Liu,Eric Nalisnick,Gillian K. Hadfield*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 通过多agent社区辩论研究LLM规范性能力，涉及多agent交互与规范推断，可作一般agent机制跟踪。

TL;DR: 论文提出多智能体社区辩论环境，将规范性能力从预训练知识中分离出来评估，发现LLM智能体难以学习并遵循社区规范，且会无选择地模仿非规范噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使AI系统与人类社区规范对齐是核心挑战，而规范数量庞大、变化迅速且常具任意性，因此对齐需要规范性能力，即仅从互动中推断社区所执行的规范，而非依赖静态预训练知识。

Method: 构建由合成规范控制辩论权限的多智能体社区辩论设置，以隔离预训练暴露来研究规范性能力；并测试多种规范性模块（用于规范推断的架构组件），考察规范风格与驱动模块的模型对规范遵循的影响。

Result: 基线LLM智能体即使学习规范能提高准确率也未能学会规范；规范遵循高度依赖规范风格和规范性模块所用模型，表明泛化性不足；当真实规范伴随特异性非规范行为时，智能体表现出无选择性归因失败，会不加区分地复制噪声，即使明确惩罚模仿不必要行为仍持续存在。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在LLM中操作化并评估规范性能力，表明当前AI系统擅长行为模仿，但缺乏辨别社会执行秩序的能力。

中文摘要：人类社区由规范性系统所治理：这些共享标准产生规定可接受行为的规范，并通过社区制裁加以执行。使日益自主的AI系统与这些规范保持一致是一项核心对齐挑战，而规范的庞大数量、快速变化以及常常任意性（例如着装或语言惯例）使这一挑战更加复杂。因此，对齐需要规范性能力：即仅从互动中辨别一个社区执行哪些规范，而不依赖静态预训练知识的能力。我们引入一种多智能体社区辩论设置，其中参与辩论的权限由合成规范所治理，以便在与预训练暴露相隔离的条件下研究规范性能力。我们表明，基线LLM智能体无法学会规范，即使这样做会提高其准确率。随后，我们尝试各种规范性模块——用于规范推断的架构组件——发现遵循规范对规范风格以及驱动规范性模块的模型都高度敏感，这表明缺乏泛化性。此外，当特异性的、非规范行为与真实规范同时出现时，LLM智能体表现出无选择性的归因失败：它们不加区分地将特异性噪声与已执行规则一起复制，即使明确惩罚模仿不必要行为，这一模式仍然持续存在。据我们所知，我们的工作是首个在LLM中操作化并评估规范性能力的工作，表明当前AI系统擅长行为模仿，但缺乏辨别社会执行秩序的能力。

Original abstract: Human communities are governed by normative systems: shared standards that produce \textit{norms} dictating acceptable behavior, enforced through community sanctioning. Aligning increasingly autonomous AI systems with these norms is a central alignment challenge, complicated by the fact that norms are vast in number, change quickly, and are often arbitrary (e.g., dress or language conventions). Thus, alignment requires \textit{normative competence}: the ability to discern from interaction alone what norms a community enforces without relying on static pretrained knowledge. We introduce a multi-agent community debate setting, where access to debate is governed by synthetic norms, to study normative competence in isolation from pretraining exposure. We show that baseline LLM agents fail to learn norms even when doing so would improve their accuracy. We then experiment with various \textit{normative modules} -- architectural components for norm inference -- finding that norm-following is highly sensitive to both the style of norm and the model powering the normative module, suggesting a lack of generalizability. Furthermore, when idiosyncratic, non-normative behaviors accompany the true norm, LLM agents exhibit an unselective attribution failure: they indiscriminately copy idiosyncratic noise alongside enforced rules, a pattern that persists even when imitating unnecessary behaviors is explicitly penalized. To the best of our knowledge, our work is the first to operationalize and evaluate normative competence in LLMs, demonstrating that current AI systems excel at behavioral mimicry but lack the capacity to discern socially enforced order.

</details>


### [8] [StoreBench: A Live-Commerce Environment for Evaluating and Training Autonomous Operator Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10942)
*Daksh Raghuvanshi,Ved Vedere,Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 22 pages, 4 figures, 8 tables

Selection reason: 提出长程商业运营agent环境与训练基准，强调工具使用、经济判断和RL后训练，可迁移至agent评测与训练。

TL;DR: 提出 StoreBench，一个生产级实时电商智能体基准：用 29 种商家工具、动态市场与校准奖励评估 LLM 的长时程经济决策；七个前沿模型均未超过脚本化启发式，人类专家仍领先，且 GRPO 后训练能显著提升小模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准多为静态：世界不主动演化、奖励仅终局判定、通过门槛任意设定，难以评估 LLM 在真实动态商业环境中的长时程规划与不确定性决策能力。

Method: 构建实时商业环境 StoreBench，智能体运营在线服装店，通过 29 种人类操作员工具行动；采用窗口化操作预算使模拟时间由动作决定；设置昼夜订单、供应商重新定价/失败、市场冲击；通过脚本化锚定策略校准阈值，加固奖励抵御奖励黑客，并保证动作序列可复现。评估 7 个前沿 LLM、11 个场景、3 个世界种子，并进行 GRPO 后训练实验。

Result: 最佳模型 DeepSeek-V4-Pro 平均通过 49% 任务-种子单元，低于脚本化智能分诊策略的 97%；人类专家平均综合得分 0.708，高于所有模型（0.700）。完整模拟年度下多数模型显著提升；Qwen3.5-27B 仅用 5 个不相交任务 GRPO 后训练，留出任务平均综合得分从 0.136 升至 0.373。

Conclusion: StoreBench 表明当前 LLM 在动态、长时程商业决策中仍落后于脚本化策略与人类专家，但后训练与更长模拟周期可带来显著提升；发布部分任务、轨迹与工具，保留完整环境以避免基准污染。

中文摘要：强化学习环境现已成为后训练阶段提升大语言模型（LLM）能力的主要手段，然而大多数智能体基准仍是静态的：世界仅在智能体行动时才推进，奖励是终局判定，通过标准被任意设定。我们提出 StoreBench，一个实时商业环境，其中智能体在生产级商业后端上运营一家中等规模的在线服装店，用以测试长时程规划与不确定性下的经济判断。顾客全天候下单，供应商重新定价并可能失败，市场冲击在部分预警或无预警下到来。智能体通过人类操作员会使用的同样 29 种商家工具采取行动，并处于窗口化操作预算之下，该预算使模拟时间成为所采取行动的函数，因此模型延迟不能影响模拟时间。通过阈值根据脚本化锚定策略进行校准，奖励已针对一系列奖励黑客手段加以加固，并且在给定一个动作序列时每个回合都能完全相同地重放。我们在匹配推理努力下，跨三个世界种子，评估了七个前沿 LLM 在 11 个为期 30 至 45 天的场景以及一个完整模拟年度上的表现。平均而言，没有模型能匹配脚本化的智能分诊策略：最佳模型 DeepSeek-V4-Pro 通过了 49% 的任务-种子单元，而该启发式策略为 97%。使用相同工具和预算工作的人类专家得分高于所有模型（平均综合得分 0.708 vs. 0.700）。在 Claude Code 测试框架下经历完整模拟年度后，大多数模型表现出显著性能提升。在一次 GRPO 后训练运行中，仅在五个互不相交任务上训练的 Qwen3.5-27B 将其在留出评估任务上的平均综合得分从 0.136 提高到 0.373。我们发布了五个示例训练集任务、十条样本轨迹以及评分和验证工具；完整环境和评估套件暂不公开，以保持基准未被污染。

Original abstract: Reinforcement learning environments are now a primary lever for improving large language model (LLM) capabilities in post-training, yet most agentic benchmarks remain static: the world moves only when the agent acts, the reward is a terminal verdict, and the pass bar is set arbitrarily. We introduce StoreBench, a live-commerce environment in which an agent runs a mid-size online apparel store on a production-grade commerce backend, testing long-horizon planning and economic judgment under uncertainty. Customers order around the clock, suppliers reprice and fail, and market shocks arrive with partial or no warning. The agent acts through the same 29 merchant tools a human operator would use, under a windowed operation budget that makes simulated time a function of actions taken, so model latency cannot influence simulated time. Pass thresholds are calibrated against scripted anchor policies, the reward is hardened against a catalogue of reward hacks, and every episode replays identically given a sequence of actions. We evaluate seven frontier LLMs on 11 scenarios of 30 to 45 days and a full simulated year, over three world seeds at matched reasoning effort. No model matches the scripted smart-triage policy on average: the best, DeepSeek-V4-Pro, passes 49% of task-seed cells against the heuristic's 97%. Human experts working through the same tools and budgets outscore every model (mean composite 0.708 vs. 0.700). Over a full simulated year under the Claude Code harness, most models show dramatic performance improvement. In a GRPO post-training run, Qwen3.5-27B trained on only five disjoint tasks raises its mean composite on the held-out evaluation tasks from 0.136 to 0.373. We release five example training-split tasks, ten sample trajectories, and the scoring and verification tooling; the full environment and evaluation suite are withheld to keep the benchmark uncontaminated.

</details>


### [9] [Learning How to Search for Plans with Exponentially Less Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10954)
*Dominik Drexler,Simon Ståhlberg,Markus Fritzsche,Blai Bonet*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 用LLM在反例引导循环中学习搜索控制策略，对自动规划与agent搜索式推理有迁移潜力。

TL;DR: 论文提出为规划学习索引式策略并加入 choose 规则，使结构终止性保证每次执行长度多项式有界；深度优先过程无需访问状态表即可在多项式空间中求解，时间复杂度仅随选择深度指数增长。用语言模型反例引导学习此类策略，在 IPC 2023 Learning Track 和 Autoscale Agile 上求解 1,890 个任务中的 1,709 个，超过 LAMA、BFWS、Levitron，且大多在一秒和 100 MiB 内完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 启发式搜索规划可能存储指数多状态，即使启发式近乎完美；需要学习搜索控制，以受限搜索避免状态爆炸，并保持可证明终止与复杂度保证。

Method: 为每个领域学习索引式策略，包含寄存器和模式；引入 choose 规则加载对象并标记回溯点，其他规则无需搜索且对所有结果有效；用深度优先过程在多项式空间中求解；用语言模型在反例引导循环中学习策略，循环证明终止、验证训练任务并控制选择深度。

Result: 证明结构终止性排除无限执行并将每次执行限制为对象数量的多项式；可求解类属于 NP，常数选择深度下属于 P；时间仅在选择深度上指数增长。实验求解 IPC 2023 Learning Track 和 Autoscale Agile 测试套件 1,890 个任务中的 1,709 个，超过 LAMA、BFWS、Levitron，多数一秒和 100 MiB 内完成。

Conclusion: 学习索引式策略结合 choose 规则可实现多项式空间规划，并在标准基准上优于经典启发式规划器，表明语言模型可学习带终止与复杂度保证的搜索控制策略。

中文摘要：对规划进行启发式搜索可能存储指数多个状态，即使其启发式几乎完美。我们转而学习搜索控制，每个领域一个规范，写为索引式策略：一种广义策略，带有保存对象的寄存器和对其规则进行排序的模式。我们加入 choose 规则，它将一个对象加载到寄存器中并标记一个回溯点，在该点一个候选就足够；所有其他规则必须对其所有结果都有效，且无需搜索。我们的主要结果是，排除无限执行的结构终止性也将每次执行限制为对象数量的多项式。然后，深度优先过程可在多项式空间中找到规划，无论状态空间多大，且无需已访问状态列表。代价是时间，仅在选择深度上呈指数增长，选择深度是沿一次执行的真实选择数量。因此，任何此类策略可求解的类都属于 NP，并在常数选择深度下属于 P。我们使用语言模型在反例引导的循环中学习这些策略，该循环证明终止性、验证训练任务，并保持选择深度较小。使用学习到的策略，该过程求解了 IPC 2023 学习赛道和 Autoscale Agile 测试套件的 1,890 个测试任务中的 1,709 个，多于 LAMA、BFWS 和 Levitron，且其中大多数在一秒和 100 MiB 内完成。

Original abstract: Heuristic search for a plan can store exponentially many states, even when its heuristic is almost perfect. We instead learn search control, one specification per domain, written as an indexical policy: a generalized policy with registers that hold objects and modes that sequence its rules. We add the choose rule, which loads an object into a register and marks a backtracking point, where one candidate suffices; every other rule must work for all of its outcomes and needs no search. Our main result is that structural termination, which rules out infinite executions, also bounds every execution by a polynomial in the number of objects. A depth-first procedure then finds a plan in polynomial space, however large the state space, with no list of visited states. The cost is time, exponential only in the choice depth, the number of real choices along an execution. Any class that such a policy solves therefore lies in NP, and in P at constant choice depth. We learn these policies with a language model in a counterexample-guided loop that certifies termination, verifies the training tasks, and keeps the choice depth small. With the learned policies, the procedure solves 1,709 of 1,890 test tasks of the IPC 2023 Learning Track and the Autoscale Agile suite, more than LAMA, BFWS, and Levitron, and most of them within one second and 100 MiB.

</details>


### [10] [Curating Always-Loaded Context for LLM Agents: A Capacitated Assortment Model with Censored Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11007)
*Zexuan Liu,Yuning Yang,Tiancheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 将常驻上下文文件策展建模为容量约束选择问题，直接对应agent记忆/harness上下文优化。

TL;DR: 该工作将 LLM 智能体上下文文件管理建模为带容量约束的选品问题，分析追加式增长的缺陷、反馈删失下的增删决策，并给出最优文件大小上界、证据收集与遗憾界；实验表明无关规则会降低模型遵从度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中上下文文件常通过追加不断增长，导致 token 重复计费、性能下降；需要理解如何策展指令并指导增删。

Method: 将上下文策展形式化为容量约束选品；在有限注意力、每会话设置成本和删失反馈下进行理论分析，并开展实证实验。

Result: 证明最优文件大小上界与候选指令数无关；追加所有正独立价值指令可能远劣于最优子集；token 预算限制低估价格损失；删除被忽略指令可能移除有用指令；刻画添加前证据量；有限审核编辑下给出遗憾界；真实文件中无关规则降低遵从度。

Conclusion: 追加式上下文管理并非最优，应按容量、证据和反馈谨慎选择指令子集，以避免无关指令带来的性能与遵从度损失。

中文摘要：在每个会话开始时，LLM 智能体会加载一个固定的上下文文件，例如 AGENTS.md。文件中的每个已加载 token 在会话后续每一轮都会再次被计费，而且这些文件随着规模增长可能会降低性能。然而在实践中，人类或自动化管理者通常通过追加来扩充这些文件。
我们将上下文策展形式化为带容量约束的选品问题。指令在有限的注意力容量下消耗 token；添加一条指令永远不会提高其他指令的遵从度，而被保留的指令会产生每个会话的设置成本。
我们证明了最优文件大小的上界，且该上界与可用候选指令数量无关；并证明追加所有具有正独立价值的指令，其净价值可能任意差于选择最优子集。当 token 价格被低估时，token 预算也限制了损失。
随后，我们考察可以从过去会话中学到什么，以及这些信息如何指导添加或删除指令的决策。反馈本质上是删失的：已加载指令的收益和危害可被观测，而缺失指令仅在其缺失造成危害时才产生反馈。
在此设定下，我们表明删除被智能体忽略的指令可能不可避免地移除有帮助的指令。我们刻画了在添加一条指令之前应收集多少证据。此外，当人类审核者每周期只能检查有限数量的编辑时，我们给出了遗憾界。实证实验进一步表明，从真实上下文文件中抽取的无关规则会降低语言模型的遵从度。

Original abstract: At the start of every session, LLM agents load a fixed context file, such as $\texttt{AGENTS.md}$. Each loaded token in the file is charged again in every later round of the session, and these files can degrade performance as they grow in size. However, in practice, human or automated curators usually grow these files by appending.
  We formulate context curation as a capacitated assortment problem. Instructions consume tokens under a finite attention capacity; adding an instruction never raises the compliance of the others, while retained instructions incur a per-session setup cost.
  We prove an upper bound on the optimal file size, regardless of the number of available candidate instructions, and that appending every instruction with positive standalone value can be arbitrarily worse in net value than selecting an optimal subset. A token budget also limits the loss when the token price is underestimated.
  We then examine what can be learned from past sessions and how this information can guide decisions to add or remove instructions. Feedback is inherently censored: the benefits and harms of loaded instructions are observable, whereas missing instructions generate feedback only when their absence causes harm.
  In this setting, we show that deleting instructions ignored by agents can inevitably remove helpful ones. We characterize how much evidence should be collected before adding an instruction. Besides, we bound regret when human reviewers can inspect only a limited number of edits per period. Empirical experiments further show that irrelevant rules drawn from real context files reduce language-model compliance.

</details>


### [11] [AgentHorizon: Evaluating Agentic Judges for Long-Horizon Computer-Use Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11050)
*Xing Han Lù,Dheeraj Vattikonda,Sina Hajimiri,Fatemeh Pesaran Zadeh,Parishad BehnamGhader,Ghazwa Darwiche,Amirhossein Kazemnejad,Christopher Pal,Alexandre Drouin,Siva Reddy*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 构建长程computer-use任务的agentic judge基准，对agent轨迹验证、评测与可靠判定有直接价值。

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentHorizon 基准，用配对的指令-轨迹对评估自动评判器在长跨应用计算机使用任务中判断成功与失败的能力，发现最佳智能体式评判器 GPT-5.5 在 AH 上达到 80.9% 平衡准确率，且工具使用、模型类型与评判器行为差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体在跨多应用长任务中的可靠性尚不明确；轨迹可能表面完成，却违反指令约束或引入副作用，因此需要能结合用户指令检查轨迹的自动评判器和相应基准。

Method: 构建 AgentHorizon：从 166 小时、三种操作系统中的人类录制轨迹中整理 1,373 个指令-轨迹对；通过为相近指令录制轨迹并交换指令构造负任务，形成配对评估，考察评判器能否区分成功轨迹与完成相似但不兼容请求的轨迹；发布 AH、AH-S、AH-D 三个划分；评估 11 种评判器，方法包括直接传入最多 300 个截图和动作的完整轨迹，以及在五种智能体框架中作为编码智能体使用。

Result: 最佳智能体式评判器 GPT-5.5 在 AH 子集上达到 80.9% 平衡准确率；工具使用提升部分模型但降低开放权重模型表现；不同评判器在接收有效轨迹与拒绝失败轨迹上的能力差异很大。

Conclusion: 结果表明，长交互历史中的成功证据常被隐藏，亟需能定位并验证这些证据的评判器，以可靠评估计算机使用智能体。

中文摘要：计算机使用智能体能够完成复杂任务，这增加了使用自动评判器来判断任务是否成功的做法，无论是用于训练还是在无人工参与的情况下用于评估。尽管这些智能体具有灵活性，但它们在跨多个应用的长时间任务中的可靠性仍不明确。一条轨迹由一长串屏幕截图和动作组成，可能看起来已经完成，但实际上违反了指令中的约束，或引入了不必要的副作用。为了识别这些错误，评判器需要结合用户指令来检查轨迹。为此，我们提出了 AgentHorizon，这是一个包含 1,373 个计算机使用任务（指令-轨迹对）的基准，取自 166 小时、跨越三种操作系统的人类录制轨迹。通过为密切相关的指令录制轨迹，我们可以通过交换指令来构造负任务。这种配对设计用于评估评判器区分成功轨迹与完成了相似（但不兼容）请求的轨迹的能力。我们以三个划分发布该基准：前沿划分 AgentHorizon（AH）、简化划分 AgentHorizon-Simple（AH-S）和开发划分 AgentHorizon-Development（AH-D）。我们进一步评估了十一种评判器：(1) 直接传入完整轨迹（最多包含 300 个屏幕截图和动作），以及 (2) 在五种智能体框架中将其用作编码智能体。我们发现，我们最好的智能体式评判器 GPT-5.5 在 AH 子集上达到了 80.9% 的平衡准确率。我们发现，工具使用改善了某些模型，但导致开放权重模型性能更差；并且不同评判器在接受有效轨迹和拒绝失败轨迹方面的能力差异巨大。我们的发现凸显了需要能够定位并验证任务已在长交互历史中正确完成的、往往隐藏的证据的评判器。

Original abstract: Computer-use agents are capable of completing complex tasks, increasing the use of automatic judges to determine success, either for training or for evaluation without human involvement. Despite their flexibility, their reliability on long tasks spanning multiple applications remains unclear. A trajectory, composed of long sequences of screenshots and actions, may appear complete, but in reality violates constraints from the instruction or introduces an unwanted side effect. To identify these errors, a judge needs to examine the trajectory with respect to the user's instruction. To this end, we introduce AgentHorizon, a benchmark of 1,373 computer-use tasks (instruction-trajectory pairs) drawn from 166 hours of human-recorded trajectories spanning three operating systems. By recording trajectories for closely related instructions, we can construct negative tasks by swapping the instructions. This paired design evaluates judges on their ability to distinguish a successful trajectory from one that completed a similar (but incompatible) request. We release the benchmark under three splits: a frontier split, AgentHorizon (AH), a simplified split, AgentHorizon-Simple (AH-S), and a development split, AgentHorizon-Development (AH-D). We further evaluate eleven judges by (1) directly passing the full trajectory (with up to 300 screenshots and actions), and (2) using them as coding agents across five agent harnesses. We find that our best agentic judge, GPT-5.5, achieves 80.9% balanced accuracy on the AH subset. We find that tool-use improves certain models but results in worse performance for open-weight models, and that judges differ drastically in their ability to accept a valid trajectory and reject failed ones. Our findings highlight the need for judges that are capable of locating and verifying often hidden evidence that a task was properly completed inside long interaction histories.

</details>


### [12] [When Interfaces Speak: Data-Aware Generative UI Harness for Active Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11123)
*Xiaolong Li,Xiaohan Xu,Jinyang Li,Xinnuo Xu,Ge Qu,Nan Huo,Jack Williams,Reynold Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 35 pages, 7 figures. Code: https://github.com/bird-bench/GenUI-Agent

Selection reason: 提出多agent生成式UI harness、工具agent与GUI coder协作及奖励审计，可迁移到agent工具编排与验证。

TL;DR: 提出GenUI-Harness多智能体框架，通过生成结构化前端UI改善人-智能体交互，并配套Dynamic UX、Reward Auditor与UI-TAU Bench；在Lite上Pass@3平均提升4.48个百分点，4B模型从9.33%提升至58.00%，平均对话轮数由3.4降至1.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人-智能体交互以文本为主，自然语言在复杂任务中会造成认知过载、歧义、信息混乱和输入缓慢；临时性生成式UI能以结构化信息引导用户完成任务。同时，用强化学习训练交互式UI生成器面临可验证奖励执行成本高和LLM-as-a-Judge奖励易被黑客攻击的问题。

Method: 提出GenUI-Harness多智能体框架，将负责信息检索与任务执行的Tool Agent与识别歧义并生成结构化界面前端代码的GUI Coder Agent配对；用Dynamic UX在单个沙箱中支持动态交互与奖励收集，用Reward Auditor监控奖励分布并将诊断模式提炼为共享评分标准和评分规范；构建UI-TAU Bench，基于10个公共数据源的真实领域数据库和Tau-Bench工具使用设置，提供Lite（300任务）和Full（1,000任务）划分。

Result: GenUI-Harness在Lite上相较smolagents取得平均4.48个百分点的Pass@3提升；训练4B骨干模型后Pass@3从9.33%提升至58.00%，超过Claude Opus 5的46.67%；在歧义和非歧义查询上保持稳健；评审者调查中，生成UI将平均对话轮数从3.4降至1.2。

Conclusion: 数据感知的生成式界面能够支持有效完成任务，并在所评估的数据库支持流程中减少对话轮数，表明其可改善复杂任务中的人-智能体交互效率。

中文摘要：当今大多数人-智能体交互仍基于文本。自然语言可能给复杂任务带来认知过载、歧义、信息混乱和输入缓慢；而临时性生成式UI可以呈现结构化信息并引导用户完成任务。我们提出GenUI-Harness，一种多智能体框架，将用于信息检索和任务执行的Tool Agent与GUI Coder Agent配对，后者识别歧义并生成用于结构化界面的前端代码。用强化学习训练该编码器具有挑战性：交互式UI生成的可验证奖励需要高昂的执行成本，而LLM-as-a-Judge奖励容易发生奖励黑客。我们通过Dynamic UX解决第一个挑战，这是一个轻量级包，用于在单个沙箱中进行动态交互和奖励收集；并通过Reward Auditor解决第二个挑战，这是一种元奖励机制，监控奖励分布并将诊断模式提炼为共享评分标准和评分规范。我们提出UI-TAU Bench，一个通过生成UI代码进行主动人-智能体交互的基准，构建于10个由公共数据源构建的真实世界领域数据库之上，并基于Tau-Bench工具使用设置，包含Lite（300个任务）和Full（1,000个任务）划分。GenUI-Harness在Lite上相较smolagents取得平均4.48个百分点的Pass@3提升。使用GenUI-Harness训练将4B骨干模型从9.33%提升至58.00% Pass@3，超过Claude Opus 5（46.67%）等更大的前沿模型。GenUI-Harness在歧义和非歧义查询上也保持稳健。在一项比较沟通渠道的评审者调查中，生成的UI将平均对话轮数从3.4轮减少至1.2轮。这些结果表明，数据感知的生成式界面可以支持有效完成任务，并在所评估的数据库支持的流程中减少对话轮数。

Original abstract: Most human-agent interaction today remains text-based. Natural language can impose cognitive overload, ambiguity, information chaos, and slow input for complex tasks; ephemeral generative UIs can present structured information and guide users toward task completion. We propose GenUI-Harness, a multi-agent harness pairing a Tool Agent for information retrieval and task execution with a GUI Coder Agent that identifies ambiguities and generates front-end code for structured interfaces. Training the coder with reinforcement learning is challenging: verifiable rewards for interactive UI generation require costly execution, while LLM-as-a-Judge rewards are prone to reward hacking. We address the first challenge with Dynamic UX, a lightweight package for dynamic interaction and reward collection in a single sandbox, and the second with Reward Auditor, a meta-reward mechanism that monitors reward distributions and distills diagnostic patterns into a shared rubric and scoring specification. We introduce UI-TAU Bench, a benchmark for active human-agent interaction through generated UI code, built on 10 real-world domain databases constructed from public data sources and based on Tau-Bench tool-use settings, with Lite (300 tasks) and Full (1,000 tasks) splits. GenUI-Harness achieves an average Pass@3 gain of 4.48 percentage points over smolagents on Lite. Training with GenUI-Harness improves a 4B backbone from 9.33% to 58.00% Pass@3, outperforming larger frontier models such as Claude Opus 5 (46.67%). GenUI-Harness also remains robust on ambiguous and non-ambiguous queries. In a reviewer survey comparing communication channels, generated UIs reduce average dialogue rounds from 3.4 to 1.2. These results show that data-aware generative interfaces can support effective task completion and reduce dialogue rounds in evaluated database-backed workflows.

</details>


### [13] [What to Admit and How to Present: Governing Persistent Memory in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11188)
*Chang Liu,Deliang Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Under review at AAMAS 2027

Selection reason: LLM agent持久记忆的准入与呈现治理，涉及记忆选择、安全与跨域泄漏，对agent记忆模块有直接参考。

TL;DR: 该研究将LLM智能体持久记忆治理拆分为准入与呈现，提出FC和PS两种推理时方法，并在多个骨干模型和外部基准上验证：改进准入可降低跨域泄漏和部分失败率，但两种方法都增加个性化失败，呈现方式差异不稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆虽能提升LLM智能体个性化，但会带来谄媚和跨域泄漏等风险；需要明确记忆治理中准入与呈现两类决策的独立作用，并检验能否在不重训练的情况下提升安全性且保留有益记忆使用。

Method: 提出并实现两种无需重训练的推理时准入设计：FC评估整个记忆条目，PS将条目分解为类型化单元，二者用确定性策略把裁定属性转为资格决策；在四骨干开发套件和含四个任务（每任务300样本）的外部基准上评估，比较逐字注入、查询条件门控、随机/相关性选择等基线，并分别控制准入与呈现做消融和统计校正。

Result: 相对于逐字注入，FC和PS将外部基准汇总裁判评估失败率降低6.7和8.8个百分点（p=2.7e-7、4.1e-12），开发集跨域泄漏最多下降29.5个百分点；查询条件门控无显著变化。匹配准入预算下，PS在Holm校正后优于随机和相关性选择；固定呈现时收紧准入再降跨域失败17.5个百分点（p=1.6e-4），而相同裁定输出的不同呈现无稳健差异。两种设计均增加个性化失败，PS未达预注册的改进与个性化保持标准。

Conclusion: 应分别评估准入与呈现：准入选择质量可带来任务特定的安全增益，但如何同时保留有益记忆使用仍未解决。

中文摘要：持久记忆可以改善LLM智能体的个性化，但也可能诱发谄媚和跨域泄漏。我们区分两类治理决策：准入，决定哪些被召回信息进入工作上下文；呈现，决定已准入信息如何表达。我们实现了两种无需重新训练的推理时设计：因子编译准入（FC），评估整个记忆条目；权限语义准入（PS），将条目分解为类型化单元；两者均通过确定性策略将裁定属性转化为资格决策。我们在一个四骨干模型开发套件和一个外部基准上进行评估，后者包含四个任务，每个任务300个样本。相对于逐字注入，FC和PS将外部基准上汇总的裁判评估失败率分别降低6.7和8.8个百分点（p = 2.7e-7和4.1e-12），开发集跨域泄漏最多下降29.5个百分点。查询条件门控基线在客观事实失败或汇总失败上未显示显著变化。在匹配的准入预算下，经Holm校正后，PS在外部客观事实判断上优于随机选择和基于相关性的选择。固定呈现方式时，收紧准入进一步将跨域失败减少17.5个百分点（p = 1.6e-4）；相比之下，对于相同裁定输出的两种呈现之间的比较，没有任何比较能在多重比较校正后仍然成立。两种设计都增加个性化失败，并且PS未达到预注册的改进和个性化保持标准。这些结果支持分别评估准入和呈现：选择质量提供了任务特定的安全增益，而保持有益记忆使用仍未解决。

Original abstract: Persistent memory can improve personalization in LLM agents but can also induce sycophancy and cross-domain leakage. We distinguish two governance decisions: admission, which determines what recalled information enters the working context, and presentation, which determines how admitted information is expressed. We implement two inference-time designs without retraining: factor-compiled admission (FC), which assesses whole memory entries, and permission-semantic admission (PS), which decomposes entries into typed units; both translate adjudicated attributes into eligibility decisions via deterministic policies. We evaluate on a four-backbone development suite and an external benchmark with four tasks of 300 samples each. Relative to verbatim injection, FC and PS reduce pooled judge-assessed failure rates on the external benchmark by 6.7 and 8.8 percentage points (p = 2.7e-7 and 4.1e-12), and development-set cross-domain leakage falls by up to 29.5 percentage points. A query-conditioned gating baseline shows no significant change in objective-fact failure or pooled failure. Under matched admission budgets, PS outperforms random and relevance-based selection on external objective-fact judgment after Holm correction. Holding presentation fixed, tightening admission cuts cross-domain failure by a further 17.5 percentage points (p = 1.6e-4); in contrast, no comparison between two renderings of identical adjudicated outputs survives multiple-comparison correction. Both designs increase personalization failures, and PS misses the preregistered improvement and personalization-preservation criteria. These results support evaluating admission and presentation separately: selection quality provides task-specific safety gains, while preserving beneficial memory use remains unresolved.

</details>


### [14] [Harness Compilation: Which Decisions Should a Small Vision-Language Model Keep?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11231)
*Minhao Fan,Yinyi Liu,Jiayu Zhao,Zihan Teng,Song Chen,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 58 pages, 10 figures, including appendices

Selection reason: 小VLM与外部harness的分工编译，含工具调用、选择、证据整合与弃权等运行时决策，对agent harness/workflow设计高度可迁移。

TL;DR: 提出离线 Harness Compilation，为冻结小型 VLM 与其外部执行框架重新分配工作；无需权重更新或教师调用，在七个 VQA 设置中比裸学生提升 9.9–23.9 分，并揭示不同决策类型与迁移特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型 VLM 能读外部证据却难以主动获取；需要按实测行为决定哪些工作留给学生、哪些交给外部框架，而非统一移除决策。

Method: 用大型教师模型根据学生执行轨迹修订可复用的内容与控制，用独立验证集选择部署的 harness；部署时不更新权重、不调用教师，并可按新学生重新编译。

Result: 在七个 VQA 设置、≤9B 学生上平均提升 9.9–23.9 分；事实卡片对十个学生均有帮助，决策策略迁移不均衡；100 练习样本时超过仅答案 LoRA；更大训练预算可匹配/超过固定 harness，二者结合在 SlideVQA 上更优。

Conclusion: 应依据实测学生行为分配决策工作，使外部框架承担不擅长或高代价环节，同时保留学生可胜任的有界选择与阅读任务。

中文摘要：小型视觉语言模型也许能够阅读外部证据，却难以获取它。我们提出 Harness Compilation（HC），一种离线流程，用于调整冻结的小型 VLM 与其外部执行框架之间的分工。一个大型教师模型利用学生的执行轨迹来修订可复用的内容与控制，而一个单独的验证集选择所部署的执行框架。部署既不需要权重更新，也不需要调用教师模型。在七个视觉问答设置中，学生模型参数至多 9B，HC 相比未加框架的学生模型将分数提高 9.9–23.9 分，这是对每个设置三次独立构建取平均的结果。对五种运行时决策类型（调用、选择、参数生成、证据整合与弃权）的干预表明了这种分配为何重要：请求证据和生成开放式查询可能代价高昂，而有界选择与阅读提供的文本仍可以是有用的学生工作。事实卡片使所有十个被评估的学生模型受益，但决策策略的迁移并不均衡。当迁移的接口不再适合学生模型时，为新学生模型重新编译会有帮助。使用 100 个练习样本，HC 在三个任务上超过仅答案 LoRA。更大的训练预算可以匹配或超过固定执行框架，而将两者结合则在 SlideVQA 上比任一单独方法都有提升。这些发现支持根据实测的学生行为来分配工作，而不是统一移除决策。

Original abstract: Small vision-language models may be able to read external evidence yet struggle to obtain it. We introduce Harness Compilation (HC), an offline procedure that adapts the division of work between a frozen small VLM and its external harness. A large teacher uses student execution traces to revise reusable content and control, while a separate validation set selects the deployed harness. Deployment requires neither weight updates nor teacher calls. Across seven visual question-answering settings with students of at most 9B parameters, HC improves scores over bare students by 9.9-23.9 points, averaged over three independent builds per setting. Interventions on five runtime decision types (invocation, selection, argument generation, evidence integration and abstention) show why this allocation matters: requesting evidence and generating open queries can be costly, whereas bounded choices and reading supplied text can remain useful student work. Fact cards benefit all ten evaluated students, but decision policies transfer unevenly. Recompilation for a new student model helps when the transferred interface no longer fits the student. With 100 practice items, HC exceeds answer-only LoRA on three tasks. Larger training budgets can match or surpass a fixed harness, while combining the two improves SlideVQA beyond either alone. These findings support allocating work from measured student behavior rather than uniformly removing decisions.

</details>


### [15] [LLM-IDEA: Identifiability-Driven Experimental Agent for Autonomous Discovery of Mechanistic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11253)
*Surya Shetty,Ulisses Braga-Neto*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 自动化实验agent用可识别性判断平台期类型并决定继续搜索或换实验，对agent规划、终止与重规划有启发。

TL;DR: 提出 LLM-IDEA，用可识别性引擎区分平台期是能力极限、可在设计类内解决还是已穷尽，并在多个基准上验证其能指导闭环实验发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主科学智能体在平台期常无法判断该继续搜索还是模型不可识别，导致无效实验。

Method: 构建可识别性驱动的闭环实验智能体 LLM-IDEA，包含三分类平台期判定引擎，并在 ODEBench、DiscoverPhysics、Alien Universe 等上评估。

Result: ODEBench 62 个含自由常数系统中 60 个第 0 轮可识别；RC 电路认证穷尽；收获模型加一个初始条件可解决；复现已知判定并推荐静脉给药组；DiscoverPhysics 中 15/15 轨迹准确模型解释评分失败，p=0.012；Alien Universe 可识别协议 8/8 种子达发现深度≥3，对照 1/8。

Conclusion: 自主发现智能体可计算而非猜测平台期应增加搜索、改进同类实验，还是更换实验类型。

中文摘要：大语言模型智能体正越来越多地被部署为自主科学家，在极少人类监督下设计实验并推断机制性世界模型。然而，可识别性常被忽视：当遇到平台期时，智能体需要知道究竟是自己还不够有能力，还是模型从数据中根本不可识别——在后一种情况下，继续进行再多同类实验也无济于事。我们提出可识别性驱动实验智能体（LLM-IDEA），用于闭环发现，并配有一个可识别性引擎，该引擎返回三种平台期判定：能力极限、可在设计类内解决、或经认证已穷尽。在 ODEBench 上，62 个含自由常数的系统中有 60 个在第 0 轮即可识别；RC 电路对该协议能够运行的每一个实验都被认证为已穷尽，而一个收获模型只需增加一个初始条件即可解决。该可识别性引擎在 Lotka-Volterra、Van der Pol、Lorenz 以及一个药代动力学模型上复现了已知判定；在药代动力学模型中，它推荐了药理学家所使用的静脉给药组，并在四种观测设计中将我们自己基准的深度评分器排在最后。在 DiscoverPhysics 基准上，它发现两个公开世界，其解释评分标准奖励了一种任何合法实验都无法做出的区分；并且该基准中所有轨迹准确的模型都在解释评分中失败（15/15，而在可识别世界中为 5/9，p = 0.012）。在 Alien Universe——我们提出的一个二体测试台中，力律在一个可证明不可识别的协议和一个可识别协议之间切换——上，LLM-IDEA 在可识别协议上在 8/8 个随机种子上达到至少 3 的发现深度，而不用它时为 1/8。因此，自主发现智能体可以计算而非猜测：平台期究竟是需要更多搜索、同类中更好的实验，还是不同类型的实验。

Original abstract: Large language model agents are being increasingly deployed as autonomous scientists, designing experiments and inferring mechanistic world models with minimal human oversight. Yet identifiability is often overlooked: when a plateau is reached, the agent needs to know whether it is not yet capable enough or the model simply is not identifiable from the data, in which case no amount of further experimentation of the same kind can help. We propose the Identifiability-Driven Experimental Agent (LLM-IDEA) for closed-loop discovery with an identifiability engine that returns a three-way plateau verdict: capability limit, resolvable within the design class, or certified exhausted. On ODEBench, 60 of the 62 systems with free constants are identifiable at round 0; the RC circuit is certified exhausted for every experiment that protocol can run, and a harvesting model is resolvable by one added initial condition. The identifiability engine reproduces known verdicts on Lotka-Volterra, Van der Pol, Lorenz, and a pharmacokinetic model, where it recommends the intravenous arm pharmacologists use, and it ranks the depth scorer of our own benchmark last among four observation designs. On the DiscoverPhysics benchmark, it finds two public worlds whose explanation rubric rewards a distinction no legal experiment can make, and every model there with accurate trajectories failed the explanation grade (15 of 15, against 5 of 9 in identifiable worlds, p = 0.012). On the Alien Universe, a two-body testbed we propose in which a force law switches between a provably non-identifiable and an identifiable protocol, LLM-IDEA on the identifiable protocol reaches discovery depth at least three on 8/8 seeds versus 1/8 without it. An autonomous discovery agent can thus compute, rather than guess, whether a plateau calls for more search, a better experiment of the same kind, or a different kind of experiment.

</details>


### [16] [TaReD: Tool-Aware Recursive Decomposition for Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11268)
*Wei-Xiang Mao,Zhi-Kai Chen,De-Chuan Zhan,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 15 pages, 3 figures, 2 tables

Selection reason: 面向长程任务的工具感知递归分解，按能力层级按需发现工具，直接可迁移到coding/通用agent规划。

TL;DR: 提出工具感知递归分解（TaReD），将工具按功能关系组织为能力层次并支持按需发现；执行时递归生成与各阶段能力对齐的子任务树，在复杂现实任务上端到端成功率较基线最高提升40个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期智能体沿单一执行链交替推理与行动，在复杂任务中历史增长会遮蔽中间依赖并传播早期规划错误；递归分解虽自然，但必须考虑系统能力以确保子任务可由可用工具执行。现实工具库过大，全量注入工具描述既消耗上下文，又降低相关工具检索和任务边界识别效果。

Method: 提出工具感知的递归分解（TaReD），按功能关系将工具组织为能力层次结构；执行时智能体按需发现工具，并利用该层次结构将复杂任务递归分解为子任务树，使各层级与对应阶段所需能力对齐。

Result: 在复杂现实世界任务上的实验表明，所提方法相比对比基线将端到端任务成功率提升最多40个百分点。TaReD实现已在GitHub开源：https://github.com/WeiXiang-Mao/TaReD。

Conclusion: TaReD通过能力层次化工具组织与按需发现，实现能力对齐的递归任务分解，从而提升复杂现实任务中的端到端成功率。

中文摘要：智能体将推理与工具相结合，以与外部系统交互并完成现实世界任务。早期智能体通常沿单一执行链交替进行推理与行动。在复杂任务上，这条链会变得不可靠，因为不断增长的历史会遮蔽中间依赖关系，并使得早期规划错误传播。将复杂任务递归分解为更小的子任务提供了一种自然的解决方案，然而有效的分解必须考虑系统的能力，以便每个子任务都能由可用工具执行。然而在现实系统中，工具库可能过大，无法完整暴露。注入每个工具描述会消耗大量上下文，同时使相关工具更难检索、有用任务边界更难识别。我们提出工具感知的递归分解，它按功能关系将工具组织为能力层次结构。在执行过程中，智能体按需发现工具，并利用该层次结构将复杂任务递归分解为子任务树，其层级与每个阶段所需的能力对齐。在复杂的现实世界任务上的实验表明，所提方法相较于对比基线将端到端任务成功率提升最多40个百分点。TaReD的实现已在GitHub上提供：https://github.com/WeiXiang-Mao/TaReD。

Original abstract: Agents combine reasoning with tools to interact with external systems and complete real-world tasks. Early agents typically interleave reasoning and actions along a single execution chain. On complex tasks, this chain becomes unreliable because growing histories obscure intermediate dependencies and allow early planning errors to propagate. Recursively decomposing a complex task into smaller subtasks offers a natural solution, yet effective decomposition must account for the system's capabilities so that each subtask can be executed by the available tools. In realistic systems, however, tool libraries can be too large to expose in full. Injecting every tool description consumes substantial context while making relevant tools harder to retrieve and useful task boundaries harder to identify. We propose tool-aware recursive decomposition, which organizes tools by functional relationships into a hierarchy of capabilities. During execution, the agent discovers tools on demand and uses the hierarchy to recursively decompose a complex task into a subtask tree whose levels are aligned with the capabilities required at each stage. Experiments on complex real-world tasks show that the proposed method improves end-to-end task success rate by up to 40 percentage points over the compared baselines. The implementation of TaReD is available on GitHub: https://github.com/WeiXiang-Mao/TaReD.

</details>


### [17] [Open-ended Scientific Discovery with Possibilistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11289)
*Anita Yang,Siu Lun Chau,Tomoya Wakayama,Krikamol Muandet,Masaki Adachi*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 开放科学发现中的LLM agent形式化与可能性前沿搜索，涉及假设生成、验证与统计有效性，对长程发现型agent有一般方法启发。

TL;DR: 论文将开放式自主科学发现形式化为AASD，提出溯因效用与可能性前沿搜索算法，在保持随时有效性的同时渐近实现ε-最优，并在实验中表现强劲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs自主科学发现需在证据累积中自适应生成检验假设并保持统计有效性；现有随时有效方法可处理数据依赖假设，但开放式发现中最佳已发现假设可能仍不理想，且背景知识也可能需修正。

Method: 用可能性理论形式化AASD，定义可计算的发现进展度量——溯因效用，并提出首个AASD算法——可能性前沿搜索，保持随时有效性并在适当条件下渐近达到ε-最优溯因效用。

Result: 在合成和真实世界科学发现任务上实验显示强劲性能。

Conclusion: 该框架为开放式自主科学发现提供了可计算进展度量与具有统计保证的搜索算法，表明其在实际科学发现任务中具有潜力。

中文摘要：使用LLMs进行自主科学发现，需要在证据不断积累的过程中自适应地生成和检验假设，同时保持统计有效性。现有的随时有效方法能够处理依赖于数据的假设，但开放式发现提出了更深层的挑战：已发现的最佳假设可能仍是一堆糟糕假设中最好的那个，而更好的解释尚未被发现；与此同时，甚至诸如物理定律之类的背景知识也可能需要根据新发现进行修正。为此，我们使用可能性理论将这一问题形式化为溯因自主科学发现（AASD）。我们引入了溯因效用，这是一种可计算的发现进展度量，并提出了可能性前沿搜索，这是首个用于AASD的算法，它保持随时有效性，并在适当条件下渐近达到ε-最优的溯因效用。在合成和真实世界科学发现任务上的实验显示出强劲性能。

Original abstract: Autonomous scientific discovery with LLMs requires generating and testing hypotheses adaptively as evidence accumulates while maintaining statistical validity. Existing anytime-valid methods can handle data-dependent hypotheses, but open-ended discovery poses a deeper challenge: the best discovered hypothesis may still be the best of a bad lot, with better explanations yet undiscovered, while even background knowledge such as physical laws may require revision in light of new findings. In response, we formalize the problem as Abductive Autonomous Scientific Discovery (AASD) using possibility theory. We introduce abductive utility, a computable measure of discovery progress, and possibility frontier search, the first algorithm for AASD, which maintains anytime validity and achieves $\varepsilon$-optimal abductive utility asymptotically under suitable conditions. Experiments on synthetic and real-world scientific-discovery tasks show strong performance.

</details>


### [18] [ORDO: Operation-level Round-aware Dynamic Ordering for MIP Presolve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11294)
*Zehuan Chen,Chunhe Song*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 将MIP presolve动作顺序建模为序列生成并用执行-观察与sequence racing验证加速，类似编译器pass排序/自动优化动作序列搜索，可迁移。

TL;DR: 提出 ORDO，将 MIP 预求解规划重构为统一原子动作空间上的自回归序列生成，借助修改 SCIP 得到的执行—观测设施与序列竞速部署，在多个未见领域实现端到端零样本加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预求解对 MIP 性能影响显著，但现有基于学习的方法只优化参数配置，无法刻画动作间非交换的时间依赖；这种默认顺序在多数领域近乎唯一且在功能上必需，人为打乱同一序列会显著抬升求解时间分布尾部。

Method: 把预求解规划建模为统一原子动作空间上的自回归序列生成，决策对象由参数变为动作序列，即 ORDO 操作级轮次感知动态排序；修改 SCIP 源码加入执行与观测设施，沿原生路径注入序列并记录实际执行的动作及其轮次；部署时采用序列竞速，令候选序列并发执行并保留胜者。

Result: 在多个未见领域取得端到端零样本加速（据称是预求解动作序列上的首次），加速幅度随领域变化且不能由语料丰富度解释；加入竞速后最强领域获得最大加速。

Conclusion: 将预求解决策对象从参数配置转为动作序列可带来跨域泛化能力，而序列竞速与执行—观测设施使该框架可实际部署。

中文摘要：预求解在很大程度上影响混合整数规划（MIP）的性能，然而基于学习的方法仅优化参数配置，无法表达动作之间非交换的时间依赖关系；这类依赖关系的默认顺序在大多数领域上几乎唯一，却在功能上不可或缺：人为打乱同一序列的顺序会使求解时间分布的尾部膨胀达数倍。我们将预求解规划重新表述为在统一原子动作空间上的自回归序列生成，把决策对象转移到动作序列上；我们称该框架为 ORDO——面向 MIP 预求解的操作级轮次感知动态排序（Operation-level Round-aware Dynamic Ordering for MIP Presolve）。其收益在于跨域泛化：在多个未见领域上，它实现了端到端零样本加速——据我们所知，这是预求解动作序列上的首次——加速幅度因领域而异，且无法用语料丰富度解释；一旦加入竞速，最强领域可获得最大的加速。部署采用序列竞速（sequence racing），即候选序列并发运行并保留胜者，其支撑来自一个执行与观测设施——通过修改 SCIP 源码添加——该设施沿原生路径注入序列，并记录哪些动作实际执行、以及在哪个轮次执行。

Original abstract: Presolve strongly affects mixed-integer programming (MIP) performance, yet learning-based methods only optimize parameter configurations and cannot express the non-commutative temporal dependencies among actions, whose default order is nearly unique on most domains, yet functionally necessary: artificially shuffling the order of the same sequence inflates the tail of the solve-time distribution by up to several-fold. We recast presolve planning as autoregressive sequence generation over a unified atomic action space, moving the decision object to action sequences; we call this framework ORDO---Operation-level Round-aware Dynamic Ordering for MIP Presolve. Its payoff is cross-domain generalization: on multiple unseen domains it attains end-to-end zero-shot speedup---to our knowledge the first for presolve action sequences---varying by domain and not explained by corpus richness, the strongest domain reaching the largest speedup once racing is added. Deployment uses sequence racing, in which candidate sequences run concurrently and the winner is kept, enabled by an execution-and-observation facility, added by modifying the SCIP source, that injects sequences along the native path and records which actions actually execute and in which round.

</details>


### [19] [DuplexAgent-RSI: Recursive Harness Improvement for Full-Duplex Voice Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11299)
*Yingda Shen,Yuxiang Wang,Kunyu Feng,Qinke Ni,Jiaqi Li,Minghao Hsu,Junan Zhang,Dekun Chen,Yutong Bian,Zhizheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 全双工语音agent的模块化harness与基于交互轨迹的递归自改进，涉及多agent委派、失败诊断与修复，harness优化可迁移。

TL;DR: DuplexAgent 提出全双工协作系统与 Duplex-Harness-RSI 闭环，通过六个可编辑模块协调实时对话与异步委托，在多个基准上兼顾连续交互、复杂推理与任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音智能体需要结合全双工实时交互与异步搜索、推理、编码委托；但现有协调框架多依赖耦合启发式方法，难以基于证据进行系统性改进。

Method: 提出 DuplexAgent，将协作工作流表达为六个可编辑模块；提出 Duplex-Harness-RSI 闭环，从交互轨迹修订模块。模拟器生成定时测试对话并产生失败轨迹以定位需修复模块；Exam Planner 依据弱点和修复档案选择下一轮测试，Harness Editor 提出针对性修改，委托池中的推理 LLM 与编码智能体也参与改进循环。

Result: 在智能、智能体和双工基准上的实验显示，DuplexAgent 将连续交互与困难推理、复杂任务执行结合，在保持强中断响应的同时，口语知识和可执行工具分数优于对比的委托系统；消融表明模块化可验证循环优于初始协调框架及缺乏诊断与修复档案的重复编辑。

Conclusion: 服务于用户的能力也可改进系统协调；模块化、可验证的闭环能有效提升全双工语音智能体与异步委托的协作表现。

中文摘要：语音智能体正趋同于一种协作模式：全双工交互模型留在实时通道上，作为对话入口，而搜索、推理和编码则通过异步委托来处理。双工模型支持连续聆听和说话，但复杂推理和工具使用可能超出其能力范围。编码智能体可以规划和执行扩展任务，但其顺序式接口并不适合实时对话。将它们结合起来需要一个协调框架，负责协调任务接收、进度、取消、替换和结果交付，同时保持对话的响应性。现有协调框架通常依赖耦合的启发式方法，使其难以基于证据进行系统性改进。我们提出 DuplexAgent，一个全双工协作系统，其协调框架将该工作流表达为六个可编辑模块；以及 Duplex-Harness-RSI，一个从交互轨迹中修订这些模块的闭环。模拟器自动生成定时的测试对话，运行系统，并产生失败轨迹，以识别需要修复的协作模块。委托池中的推理 LLM 和编码智能体也服务于改进循环：Exam Planner 根据观察到的弱点和修复档案选择下一轮测试，Harness Editor 提出有针对性的模块修改。因而，服务于用户的能力也改进了系统的协调能力。在智能、智能体和双工基准上的实验表明，DuplexAgent 将连续交互与困难推理和复杂任务执行结合起来，在保持强中断响应的同时，实现了比所比较的委托系统更强的口语知识和可执行工具分数。协调框架消融进一步表明，这种模块化、可验证的循环优于初始协调框架以及缺乏其诊断和修复档案的重复编辑。

Original abstract: Voice agents are converging on a collaboration pattern: a full-duplex interaction model stays on the live channel as the entry to the conversation, while search, reasoning, and coding are handled through asynchronous delegation. A duplex model supports continuous listening and speaking, but complex reasoning and tool use may exceed its capabilities. A coding agent can plan and execute extended tasks, but its sequential interface is a poor fit for live conversation. Combining them requires a harness that coordinates task acceptance, progress, cancellation, replacement, and result delivery while keeping the conversation responsive. Existing harnesses often rely on coupled heuristics, making them difficult to improve systematically from evidence. We present DuplexAgent, a full-duplex collaboration system whose harness expresses this workflow as six editable modules, and Duplex-Harness-RSI, a closed loop that revises them from interaction traces. A simulator automatically generates timed test conversations, runs the system, and produces failure traces that identify the collaboration modules requiring repair. Reasoning LLMs and coding agents in the delegation pool also serve the improvement loop: the Exam Planner selects the next tests from observed weaknesses and the repair archive, and the Harness Editor proposes targeted module changes. The capabilities that serve the user thus also improve the system's coordination. Experiments on intelligence, agentic, and duplex benchmarks show that DuplexAgent combines continuous interaction with difficult reasoning and complex task execution, achieving stronger spoken-knowledge and executable-tool scores than the compared delegated systems while maintaining strong interruption response. A harness ablation further shows that this modular, verifiable loop outperforms the initial harness and repeated editing that lacks its diagnosis and repair archive.

</details>


### [20] [MedBenchAgent: Towards Systematic Automation of Medical VLM Benchmark Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11312)
*Yulin Fu,Junren Wang,Guangjing Yang,Zhangyuan Yu,Wanran Sun,Jiabao Zhou,Jin Yin,Qicheng Lao*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 25 pages, 6 figures

Selection reason: 多agent框架将基准规格约束编译为评估项，规划与实例化分离，对agent评测构建有一般方法参考，虽在医疗领域。

TL;DR: 提出MedBenchAgent，将医学VLM基准构建建模为受约束编译，通过多智能体与BIR从评估需求、异构标注和医学知识推导规范并实例化审计项目，任务空间F1达90.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化主要是在预定义基准规范内生成评测项，尚未解决规范本身的自动推导问题，即评估什么、哪些标注支持每个任务，以及如何将证据转化为可靠评测项。

Method: 将基准构建形式化为受约束编译；提出多智能体框架MedBenchAgent及基准中间表示BIR；把推导并验证规范的规划阶段与在锁定规范下构建并审计项目的实例化阶段分离。

Result: 任务空间F1为90.9%，优于直接任务归纳（79.2–80.0%）和先验引导归纳（85.1%）；正确识别任务的1,000个抽样项目中有994个通过人工审计；可移植至专门医学领域，评估12个VLM并揭示聚合分数掩盖的任务与设置特定差异。

Conclusion: 受约束编译可作为超越问题生成、可扩展且可审计的医学VLM基准构建框架。

中文摘要：随着标注丰富的影像数据集和大语言模型（LLMs）的出现，大规模构建医学视觉-语言模型（VLM）基准日益可行，但现有自动化主要集中于在预定义基准规范内生成评估项目。我们研究一个更广泛的问题：自动推导规范本身——评估什么、哪些标注支持每个任务，以及如何将这些证据转化为可靠的评估项目。我们将基准构建形式化为受约束的编译，其中基准规范从评估需求、异构标注和医学知识中逐步推导而来。基于这一形式化，我们提出MedBenchAgent，一个多智能体框架，带有基准中间表示（BIR），该表示跨构建阶段编码任务定义、证据映射、评估协议和项目规范。MedBenchAgent将规划（推导并验证规范）与实例化（在锁定规范下构建并审计项目）分离。MedBenchAgent实现了90.9%的任务空间F1，优于直接任务归纳（79.2–80.0%）和先验引导归纳（85.1%）；来自正确识别任务的1,000个抽样项目中有994个通过人工审计。我们进一步证明其对专门医学领域的可移植性，并评估了十二个VLM，揭示了被聚合分数掩盖的任务和设置特定差异。这些结果确立了受约束编译作为一种可扩展且可审计的框架，用于超越问题生成的医学VLM基准构建。

Original abstract: Large-scale construction of medical vision-language model (VLM) benchmarks is increasingly feasible with richly annotated imaging datasets and large language models (LLMs), yet existing automation largely focuses on generating evaluation items within predefined benchmark specifications. We study the broader problem of automatically deriving the specification itself: what to evaluate, which annotations support each task, and how to translate this evidence into reliable evaluation items. We formulate benchmark construction as constrained compilation, in which the benchmark specification is progressively derived from evaluation requirements, heterogeneous annotations, and medical knowledge. Based on this formulation, we introduce MedBenchAgent, a multi-agent framework with a Benchmark Intermediate Representation (BIR) that encodes task definitions, evidence mappings, evaluation protocols, and item specifications across construction stages. MedBenchAgent separates planning, which derives and verifies the specification, from instantiation, which constructs and audits items under the locked specification. MedBenchAgent achieves a Task-Space F1 of 90.9%, outperforming direct task induction (79.2-80.0%) and prior-guided induction (85.1%); 994 of 1,000 sampled items from correctly identified tasks pass human audit. We further demonstrate portability to a specialized medical domain and evaluate twelve VLMs, revealing task- and setting-specific variation obscured by aggregate scores. These results establish constrained compilation as a scalable and auditable framework for medical VLM benchmark construction beyond question generation.

</details>


### [21] [Mine Odyssey: Benchmarking Spatial Agentic Intelligence in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11328)
*Yuxuan Cao,Junlong Li,Hao Li,Junxian He*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 空间agent长程导航benchmark，覆盖探索、反馈更新与错误恢复，属通用agent评测与长程任务，可借鉴任务设计。

TL;DR: 提出 Mine Odyssey 基准，用 Minecraft 重建真实地点，评估智能体空间智能；包含 180 个任务、30 个地点，要求探索、导航、开门、跨层移动并纠错。八个先进模型中最高成功率为 85.6%，但开放权重模型仅 23.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准只覆盖有限的空间布局、尺度和通行要求，难以充分评估智能体在物理世界中探索陌生环境、更新空间理解并基于反馈调整行动的能力。

Method: 构建 Mine Odyssey 基准，基于 Minecraft 重建真实世界地点，覆盖五大洲 20 个国家和地区的 30 个地点，包括 20 个室外与 10 个室内场景；设计 180 个自然语言指令任务，指定访问航点及顺序，并人工验证可达性。任务要求智能体寻找可通行路线和入口、开门、使用楼梯和梯子跨层移动，同时监控进展并从导航错误中恢复。

Result: 在八个最先进模型中，GPT-6 Astra 成功率最高，为 85.6%；Claude Opus 5.5 以 73.9% 位居第二；最强开放权重模型 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 仅达到 23.9%。综合分析及消融研究揭示了当前模型的局限性。

Conclusion: 当前模型在智能体空间智能方面仍有很大改进空间，Mine Odyssey 为评估和推进该方向提供了基准与洞见。

中文摘要：基础模型的进展正在推动人们将智能体引入物理世界以协助人类。此类智能体需要具有智能体空间智能：探索陌生环境，通过交互更新空间理解，并基于反馈调整行动，以维持朝一系列目标推进的进展。现有基准仅覆盖有限范围的空间布局、尺度和通行要求。我们提出 Mine Odyssey，一个利用真实世界地点的 Minecraft 重建来评估智能体空间智能的基准。它包含 180 个任务，覆盖五大洲 20 个国家和地区的 30 个此类地点，其中包括 20 个室外和 10 个室内场景。这些场景跨越多样的空间尺度、布局、地形和连通模式，从曼哈顿中城和恩特鲁普乡村到圣卢西亚山和白金汉宫。我们选择有意义的航点，例如地标、建筑和房间，并人工验证其可达性。每个任务提供一条自然语言指令，指定要访问哪些航点以及以何种顺序访问。完成这些任务要求智能体找到可通行路线和入口、开门，并使用楼梯和梯子在楼层之间移动，同时监控自身进展并从导航错误中恢复。在评估的八个最先进模型中，GPT-6 Astra 取得最高成功率 85.6%。然而，第二好的模型 Claude Opus 5.5 完成了 73.9% 的任务，而评估的最强开放权重模型 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 达到 23.9%，这凸显了当前模型在智能体空间智能方面仍有很大的改进空间。对 Mine Odyssey 的综合分析和消融研究揭示了当前模型的局限性，并为推进智能体空间智能提供了洞见。

Original abstract: Advances in foundation models are driving efforts to introduce agents to assist people in the physical world. Such agents require agentic spatial intelligence: exploring unfamiliar environments, updating spatial understanding through interaction, and adapting actions based on feedback to sustain progress toward a sequence of goals. Existing benchmarks cover only a limited range of spatial layouts, scales, and traversal requirements. We introduce Mine Odyssey, a benchmark for evaluating agentic spatial intelligence using Minecraft reconstructions of real-world locations. It comprises 180 tasks covering 30 such locations across 20 countries and regions on five continents, including 20 outdoor and 10 indoor settings. These settings span diverse spatial scales, layouts, terrains, and connectivity patterns, from Midtown Manhattan and rural Entrup to Santa Lucía Hill and Buckingham Palace. We select meaningful waypoints, such as landmarks, buildings, and rooms, and manually verify their accessibility. Each task provides a natural-language instruction specifying which waypoints to visit and in what order. Completing these tasks requires agents to find accessible routes and entrances, open doors, and move between levels using stairs and ladders, while monitoring their progress and recovering from navigation errors. Across eight evaluated state-of-the-art models, GPT-6 Astra achieves the highest success rate of 85.6%. However, the second-best model, Claude Opus 5.5, completes 73.9% of tasks, while the strongest evaluated open-weight model, DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash, reaches 23.9%, highlighting substantial room for improvement in the agentic spatial intelligence of current models. Comprehensive analyses and ablation studies on Mine Odyssey reveal current models' limitations and provide insights for advancing agentic spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [22] [ReCast: Attribution-Oriented Step Representation Learning for LLM-Based Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11334)
*Weilin Jin,Mingyu Wang,Taiyu Zhu,Ziqi Zhou,Wenbo Li,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan,Yifan Wu,Ying Li,Zhonghai Wu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: LLM agent失败归因与步骤表征学习，定位最早根因步骤，对agent可靠性、调试与长程轨迹分析直接相关。

TL;DR: 针对LLM智能体失败归因中隐藏状态步骤表示区分根因步骤能力有限的问题，提出ReCast步骤表示学习方法及ReCast-2K数据集，在四个基准上取得最佳Hit@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 失败可能由早期步骤引发并传播，但现有利用LLM隐藏状态作为步骤表示的方法对根因步骤与其他步骤的区分能力有限，因此需要面向归因的步骤表示学习。

Method: ReCast先将冻结LLM的隐藏状态转换为面向归因的步骤表示：选择归因相关层，构建互补的模式与偏差特征，再用对比与排序目标训练编码器学习上下文化步骤表示；同时构建ReCast-2K失败归因训练数据集。

Result: 在四个基准上取得最佳Hit@1；在Who&When Algorithm和Handcrafted上分别比最强基线高5.65和9.19个百分点。

Conclusion: ReCast能有效提升失败归因性能，表明面向归因的步骤表示学习与相应数据集对定位根因步骤具有价值。

中文摘要：在基于LLM的智能体系统中，失败可能源于早期步骤，其影响会通过后续交互传播，使得其根源难以识别。为了将此类失败追溯至其根源，失败归因被形式化为识别对失败负责的最早步骤的任务。近期方法利用LLM内部信号进行失败归因，通常使用隐藏状态作为步骤表示。因此，我们进行了一项实证研究，以评估这些表示在区分根因步骤与其他步骤方面的有效性，并发现其区分能力有限。受此观察启发，我们提出ReCast，一种步骤表示学习方法，将来自冻结LLM的隐藏状态转换为面向归因的步骤表示。ReCast首先选择与归因相关的层，然后构建互补的模式和偏差特征，最后通过使用对比和排序目标训练的编码器学习上下文化步骤表示。我们还引入了ReCast-2K，一个用于失败归因的训练数据集。ReCast在四个基准上取得最佳Hit@1，在Who&When Algorithm和Handcrafted上分别超过最强基线5.65和9.19个百分点。代码可在 https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCast-5FB6 获取。

Original abstract: In LLM-based agent systems, failures can originate from early steps whose effects propagate through subsequent interactions, making their origins difficult to identify. To trace such failures back to their origin, failure attribution has been formulated as the task of identifying the earliest step responsible for the failure. Recent methods leverage LLM internal signals for failure attribution, typically using hidden states as step representations. We therefore conduct an empirical study to evaluate how effectively these representations distinguish root-cause steps from other steps and find limited separation. Motivated by this observation, we propose ReCast, a step representation learning method that transforms hidden states from a frozen LLM into attribution-oriented step representations. ReCast first selects attribution-relevant layers, then constructs complementary pattern and deviation features, and finally learns contextualized step representations through an encoder trained with contrastive and ranking objectives. We also introduce ReCast-2K, a training dataset for failure attribution. ReCast achieves the best Hit@1 across four benchmarks, surpassing the strongest baseline by 5.65 and 9.19 pp on Who&When Algorithm and Handcrafted, respectively. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCast-5FB6 .

</details>


### [23] [EvoSim: Learning to Model, Modeling to Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11344)
*Yun-Wei Song,Jinkai Tao,Jun-Dong Zhang,Rui Zhang,Yi-Min Wu,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 43 pages, 6 figures

Selection reason: 自演化多agent科研系统，用实验差异驱动机制/方程修订与编排演化，体现基于反馈的闭环自我改进，可迁移到agent优化。

TL;DR: EvoSim是一个自演化AI科学家，通过实验差异驱动机理与方程修正，并在电池建模中实现高精度预测，且自演化使模型与物理误差较基线降低约36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建基于物理的模型需选择物理过程、定义状态与控制方程、指定耦合关系、从实验辨识参数，但现有AI系统难以自主完成这些模型结构决策。

Method: 提出EvoSim，利用实验差异驱动机理和方程修正，以留出实验数据检验物理合理性；通过探索轨迹更新知识、技能与多智能体编排，实现协同演化，从而提升选择机理、诊断失败和协调研究的能力。

Result: 在两个工业电池建模任务上评估：25至45摄氏度和2 C至6 C下预测锂金属析出起始，荷电状态平均绝对误差为1.79%；车辆驾驶工况下动态电压预测均方根误差为7.62 mV，超过人类专家模型已报告精度；自演化较基线降低模型与物理误差约36%。

Conclusion: EvoSim能将实验观测转化为经过验证的模型与累积研究专业知识，并提升科学建模能力。

中文摘要：基于物理的模型将科学解释与定量预测联系起来。构建这类模型需要选择物理过程、定义状态与控制方程、指定耦合关系，并从实验中辨识参数。现有AI系统在自主做出这些模型结构决策方面仍然有限。我们提出EvoSim，一个用于物理建模的自演化AI科学家。它利用实验差异来驱动机理与方程修正，并使用留出实验数据来检验物理合理性。探索轨迹使知识、技能和多智能体编排得到更新。这种协同演化改进了基于物理的模型以及EvoSim选择机理、诊断失败和协调研究的能力。我们在两个工业电池建模任务上评估EvoSim。它预测锂金属析出起始，在25至45摄氏度和2 C至6 C条件下，荷电状态的平均绝对误差为1.79%。车辆驾驶条件下的动态电压预测实现了7.62 mV的均方根误差，超过了人类专家所开发模型的已报告精度。自演化相对于基线将模型误差和物理误差降低约36%，展示了改进的科学建模能力。EvoSim将实验观测转化为经过验证的模型和累积的研究专业知识。

Original abstract: Physics-based models connect scientific explanation with quantitative prediction. Constructing them requires selecting physical processes, defining states and governing equations, specifying couplings, and identifying parameters from experiments. Existing AI systems remain limited in making these model structure decisions autonomously. We introduce EvoSim, a self-evolving AI scientist for physical modeling. It uses experimental discrepancies to drive mechanism and equation revisions and held-out experimental data to test physical plausibility. Exploration traces make updates to knowledge, skills, and multi-agent orchestration. This co-evolution improves physics-based models and EvoSim's ability to select mechanisms, diagnose failures, and coordinate research. We evaluate EvoSim on two industrial battery modeling tasks. It predicts lithium-metal-plating onset from 25 to 45 degrees Celsius and 2 C to 6 C with a mean absolute error of 1.79% in state of charge. Dynamic voltage prediction under vehicle driving conditions achieves a root mean square error of 7.62 mV, surpassing the reported accuracy of models developed by human experts. Self-evolution reduces model and physics errors by approximately 36% relative to baseline, demonstrating improved scientific modeling capability. EvoSim turns experimental observations into validated models and cumulative research expertise.

</details>


### [24] [SynCo: Data Synthesis Co-Training for Self-Evolving LLMs via Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11345)
*Wei Yang,Shawn Li,Yuehan Qin,Yawei Wang,Mingxi Wang,Shixuan Li,Tiankai Yang,Jiate Li,Jesse Thomason,Xuezhe Ma,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 多agent强化学习实现LLM自演化，合成器与求解器协同进化训练任务分布，涉及自改进与经验复用，可启发agent训练机制。

TL;DR: 提出SynCo：用多智能体强化学习联合训练合成器与推理器，使任务分布随推理能力共同演化，在八个数学推理基准上取得最优总体表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自演化LLM智能体多依赖静态数据集或单独更新的合成模型，导致训练任务与智能体当前能力错配：旧任务变简单、难任务无信息量，从而限制持续自我改进。

Method: SynCo是一个智能体式数据合成协同训练框架，基于多智能体强化学习，联合优化独立参数化的合成器与推理器。合成器依据推理器演化中的能力状态生成任务；每个任务产生多次推理器rollout，其结果给出互补奖励：正确性反馈训练推理器，任务质量、答案可靠性和基于结果的可教性指导合成器。更新反馈到后续合成轮次，使求解策略与训练分布协同演化。

Result: 在八个数学推理基准上的大量实验显示，SynCo显著优于多种现有合成数据方法和受控基线，取得最强总体性能，且大部分增益来自先前未解决的问题。

Conclusion: SynCo表明，通过协同演化任务求解策略与其训练分布，可缓解能力与训练经验错配，推动自演化LLM实现持续自我改进。

中文摘要：自演化LLM智能体有望通过持续交互与学习实现自主提升，从而减少对人工整理监督的依赖。实现这一前景不仅需要更新智能体，还需要随着其能力变化而演化其训练经验。然而，大多数现有流程依赖静态数据集或单独更新的合成模型，导致先前有用的任务变得过于简单，而过于困难的任务则仍然缺乏信息量。智能体能力与训练经验之间日益加剧的错配限制了持续自我改进。为解决这一问题，我们提出SynCo，一种基于多智能体强化学习的、用于自演化LLM的智能体式数据合成协同训练框架。SynCo联合优化两个独立参数化的智能体：一个合成器（Synthesizer），根据推理器（Reasoner）不断演化的能力状态构建训练任务；一个推理器，从由此产生的经验中学习。每个合成任务会诱导推理器产生多次 rollout，其结果向两个智能体提供互补奖励。正确性反馈改进推理器，而任务质量、答案可靠性以及基于结果的可教性指导合成器。它们的更新被反馈到后续合成轮次中，使任务求解策略及其训练分布能够共同演化。在八个数学推理基准上的大量实验表明，SynCo显著优于广泛的现有合成数据方法和受控基线，取得了最强的总体性能，同时其大部分增益来自先前未解决的问题。

Original abstract: Self-evolving LLM agents promise to improve autonomously through continual interaction and learning, reducing their dependence on manually curated supervision. Realizing this promise requires not only updating the agent, but also evolving its training experience as its capabilities change. However, most existing pipelines rely on static datasets or separately updated synthesis models, causing previously useful tasks to become trivial while overly difficult tasks remain uninformative. This growing mismatch between agent capability and training experience limits sustained self-improvement. To address this problem, we propose SynCo, an agentic data synthesis co-training framework for self-evolving LLMs based on multi-agent reinforcement learning. SynCo jointly optimizes two independently parameterized agents: a Synthesizer that constructs training tasks from the Reasoner's evolving capability state, and a Reasoner that learns from the resulting experience. Each synthesized task induces multiple Reasoner rollouts whose outcomes provide complementary rewards to both agents. Correctness feedback improves the Reasoner, while task quality, answer reliability, and outcome-grounded teachability guide the Synthesizer. Their updates are fed back into subsequent synthesis rounds, allowing the task-solving policy and its training distribution to evolve together. Extensive experiments across eight mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that SynCo substantially outperforms a broad range of existing synthetic-data methods and controlled baselines, achieving the strongest overall performance while deriving most of its gains from previously unsolved problems.

</details>


### [25] [RIT-RAG: Navigating Document Corpora with Retrieval-Induced Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11370)
*Meghanadh Pulivarthi,Swaraj Kumar Biswal,Kushagra Bhushan,Yatin Nandwani,Sachindra Joshi,Dinesh Raghu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 24 pages (main text through Limitations ends on page 9, followed by references and appendix), 9 figures, 16 tables

Selection reason: 提出结构感知的 agentic RAG：先检索再让 LLM agent 导航子树并重写查询，其检索定位+agent 决策的规划模式可迁移到 coding agent 的上下文检索。

TL;DR: 提出 RIT-RAG，将内容检索与文档结构导航结合：检索文本块以诱导跨文档子树，再由 LLM 智能体选择性导航和读取，从而在多个基准和 EntQABench 上取得最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Agentic RAG 暴露孤立文本块且缺乏文档结构，难以区分真正相关证据与仅表面相似的块；PageIndex 等结构感知方法受 LLM 上下文限制，无法扩展到大语料库结构，且必须先锁定单个文档，无法从错误文档选择中恢复。

Method: 离线为每个文档依据目录或站点地图构建树；查询时先检索广泛文本块，利用其位置诱导可管理的子树，可跨多个文档；LLM 智能体在子树上导航、选择性读取有希望节点，并在需要时重写查询。检索负责提出查看位置，智能体负责决定读取内容。

Result: 在金融、科学和客户支持基准上，RIT-RAG 在 vanilla、基于图和 agentic 基线中答案准确率最高；在包含 284 万个技术文档网页的新基准 EntQABench 上，使用三个 LLM 时准确率相比最强基线提升 6.8 至 11.4 个百分点。

Conclusion: 检索与结构导航相结合可扩展至大语料库，并能弥补单一文档选择错误；由检索定位、智能体阅读的 RIT-RAG 显著提升了多领域 RAG 答案准确率。

中文摘要：检索增强生成（RAG）将语言模型锚定于外部语料库。Agentic RAG 支持迭代搜索，但使模型暴露于孤立文本块，缺乏文档结构，难以区分相关证据与仅与查询相似的文本块。诸如 PageIndex 之类的结构感知方法可导航文档结构，但无法扩展到大型语料库的结构，因为这些结构无法放入 LLM 上下文。因此，它们首先锁定单个文档（借助文档检索器），且无法从错误选择中恢复。我们提出 RIT-RAG（Retrieval-Induced Tree RAG，检索诱导树 RAG），将内容检索与结构导航相结合。离线时，RIT-RAG 根据每个文档的目录或站点地图为其构建一棵树。查询时，它检索一组广泛的文本块，并利用其位置诱导出可管理的子树，可能跨多个文档。LLM 智能体导航这些子树，选择性地读取有希望的节点，并在需要时重新表述查询。因此，检索提出在哪里查找，而智能体决定读取什么。在金融、科学和客户支持基准上，RIT-RAG 在 vanilla、基于图和 agentic 基线中取得了最高答案准确率。在 EntQABench（我们新的包含 284 万个技术文档网页的基准）上，它在三个 LLM 上将准确率相比最强基线提升了 6.8 至 11.4 个百分点。

Original abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) grounds language models in external corpora. Agentic RAG enables iterative search, yet exposes the model to isolated chunks without document structure, making it difficult to distinguish relevant evidence from chunks that merely resemble the query. Structure-aware methods such as PageIndex navigate document structure but cannot scale to the structures of large corpora, which do not fit in the LLM context. Hence, they first commit to a single document using a document retriever and cannot recover from a wrong choice. We propose RIT-RAG (Retrieval-Induced Tree RAG), which combines content retrieval with structural navigation. Offline, RIT-RAG builds a tree for each document from its table of contents or sitemap. At query time, it retrieves a broad set of chunks and uses their positions to induce manageable sub-trees, potentially across multiple documents. An LLM agent navigates these sub-trees, selectively reads promising nodes, and reformulates queries when needed. Thus, retrieval proposes where to look, while the agent decides what to read. Across financial, scientific, and customer-support benchmarks, RIT-RAG achieves the highest answer accuracy among vanilla, graph-based, and agentic baselines. On EntQABench, our new benchmark of 2.84 million technical-documentation webpages, it improves accuracy by 6.8 to 11.4 points over the strongest baseline across three LLMs.

</details>


### [26] [Environmental Feedback Modeling Matters: Rethinking Feedback Treatment in Agentic Hindsight Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11384)
*Hangxi Guo,Fengyuan Liu,Yue Wang,Yuhua Qi,Haoyi Xiong,Fei Sun,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 交互式语言 agent 的自蒸馏框架，显式建模环境反馈并将其与 hindsight 监督互促，对 agent 自我改进与反馈利用有直接启发。

TL;DR: SELF 联合优化环境反馈建模与事后自蒸馏，在无奖励交互环境中提升语言智能体；Qwen3-8B 上 τ-bench 成功率超 SDPO/GRPO 6.4/4.1 个百分点，AppWorld 目标完成超 10.71/3.57 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在奖励不可用时无法直接用于训练语言智能体；现有将环境反馈作为特权上下文的事后自蒸馏仅让教师以反馈为条件仍不足，需要重新思考智能体自蒸馏中环境反馈的使用方式。

Method: 提出 SELF（agentic SElf-distilLation with environmental Feedback modeling）框架，联合优化环境反馈建模与事后自蒸馏：一方面学习预测环境对智能体动作的响应，另一方面将反馈条件自教师的指导蒸馏到策略中。

Result: 分析表明环境反馈建模与自蒸馏相互增强：反馈建模强化事后监督和策略学习，自蒸馏提升反馈建模能力。Qwen3-8B 上，τ-bench 成功率分别比 SDPO 和 GRPO 高 6.4 和 4.1 个百分点；AppWorld 任务目标完成率分别高 10.71 和 3.57 个百分点。

Conclusion: SELF 在智能体自蒸馏中更有效地利用环境反馈，从而提升智能体能力。

中文摘要：强化学习常用于在交互环境中训练语言智能体，但当奖励不可用时无法直接应用。近期方法将环境反馈作为特权上下文用于事后自蒸馏，但我们的分析表明，仅仅让教师模型以反馈为条件是不够的，这促使我们重新思考在智能体自蒸馏中如何使用环境反馈。鉴于环境反馈包含丰富的监督信息，可用于建模环境如何响应智能体动作，我们提出 agentic SElf-distilLation with environmental Feedback modeling（SELF），一个联合优化环境反馈建模与事后自蒸馏的框架。SELF 学习预测环境响应，同时将来自以反馈为条件的自教师的指导蒸馏到策略中。我们的分析揭示了一种相互增强机制：环境反馈建模增强事后监督和策略学习，而自蒸馏提升模型建模环境反馈的能力。在 Qwen3-8B 上，SELF 在 τ-bench 成功率上分别超过 SDPO 和 GRPO 6.4 和 4.1 个百分点，在 AppWorld 任务目标完成率上分别超过 10.71 和 3.57 个百分点。这些结果表明，SELF 在智能体自蒸馏中更有效地利用环境反馈，从而提升智能体能力。

Original abstract: Reinforcement learning is commonly used to train language agents in interactive environments, but cannot be directly applied when rewards are unavailable. Recent methods use environmental feedback as privileged context for hindsight self-distillation, but our analysis suggests that simply conditioning the teacher on feedback is insufficient, motivating us to rethink how environmental feedback is used in agentic self-distillation. Given that environmental feedback contains rich supervision for modeling how the environment responds to agent actions, we introduce \textit{agentic SElf-distilLation with environmental Feedback modeling} (SELF), a framework that jointly optimizes environmental feedback modeling and hindsight self-distillation. SELF learns to predict environmental responses while distilling guidance from a feedback-conditioned self-teacher into the policy. Our analysis reveals a mutually reinforcing mechanism: environmental feedback modeling strengthens hindsight supervision and policy learning, while self-distillation enhances the model's ability to model environmental feedback. With Qwen3-8B, SELF outperforms SDPO and GRPO by 6.4 and 4.1 percentage points in $τ$-bench success rate, and by 10.71 and 3.57 percentage points in AppWorld task goal completion, respectively.
